<h1>Contents</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-jaya-sa-parikara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Jay Dhvani <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="verse"><strong>Jay Saparikar Śrī Śrī Guru Gaurāṅga Gāndharvā Govindasundar Jīu kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Jay Om Viṣṇupād Paramahaṁsa Parivrājakāchārya-varya Aṣṭottara-śata-śrī Śrīmad Bhakti Nirmal Āchārya Mahārāj kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Jay Om Viṣṇupād Paramahaṁsa Parivrājakāchārya-varya Aṣṭottara-śata-śrī Śrīmad Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Jay Om Viṣṇupād Paramahaṁsa Parivrājakāchārya-varya Aṣṭottara-śata-śrī Śrīmad Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Jay Om Viṣṇupād Paramahaṁsa Parivrājakāchārya-varya Aṣṭottara-śata-śrī Śrīmad Bhakti Siddhānta Sarasvatī Goswāmī Ṭhākur kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Jay Om Viṣṇupād Śrīla Gaura Kiśor Dās Bābājī Mahārāj kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Jay Om Viṣṇupād Śrīla Sachchidānanda Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Jay Om Viṣṇupād Śrīla Jagannāth Dās Bābājī Mahārāj kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Śrīla Baladev Vidyābhūṣaṇ kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Śrīla Viśvanāth Chakravartī Ṭhākur kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Śrī Narottam Śyāmānanda Śrīnivās Āchārya Prabhu-traya kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Śrīla Vṛndāvan Dās Ṭhākur kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Śrīla Kṛṣṇa Dās Kavirāj Goswāmī kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Śrī Rūpa, Sanātan, Bhaṭṭa Raghunāth, Śrī Jīva, Gopāl Bhaṭṭa, Dās Raghunāth, Ṣaḍ Goswāmī Prabhu kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Śrī Rūpānuga Guru-varga kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Namāchārya Śrīla Hari Dās Ṭhākur kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Śrīla Svarūp Dāmodar, Śrīla Rāy Rāmānandādi Śrī Gaura-śakti-varga kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Premse Kaho Śrī Kṛṣṇa Chaitanya Prabhu Nityānanda Śrī Advaita Gadādhar Śrīvāsādi Gaura-bhakta-vṛnda kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Sapārṣad Śrī Nityānanda Prabhu kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Sapārṣad Śrīman Mahāprabhu kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Śrī Nabadwīp Dhām kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Śrī Gaṅgā Yamunā kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Śrī Vṛndāvan Dhām kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa Gopa Gopī Śyām Kuṇḍa Rādhā Kuṇḍa Go Girirāj Govardhan kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Bhakti Devī Vṛndā Devī Tulasī Devī kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Śrī Puruṣottam Dhām kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Baladev Subhadrā Jagannāth Jīu kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Grantharāj Śrīmad Bhāgavatam Śrī Chaitanya-charitāmṛta kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Ākar Maṭh Rāj Śrī Chaitanya Maṭh kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Tadīya Śākhā Maṭh kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Śrī Chaitanya Sāraswat Maṭh kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Viśva-vyāpī Tadīya Śākhā Maṭh kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Ananta-koṭī Vaiṣṇava-vṛnda kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Samaveta Vaiṣṇava-vṛnda kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Hari-nām-saṅkīrtan kī jay!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>Nitāi Gaura Premānande! Haribol!</strong></p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section class="category">
<h1>Ārati</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-jaya-jaya-gurudever-arati-ujjvala" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Guru Ārati <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīpād B. A. Paramahaṁsa Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya jaya gurudever ārati ujjvala</strong><br>
<strong>govardhana-pāda-pīṭhe bhuvana-maṅgala</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to the effulgent ārati of Śrīla Gurudev taking place at the foot of Govardhan for the benefit of the world!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī bhakti sundara deva prabhu śiromaṇi</strong><br>
<strong>gosvāmī govinda jaya ānandera khani</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to the mine of joy, Śrī Bhakti Sundar Govinda DevGoswāmī, who is the crown-jewel of Gurus!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ājānu lambita bhuja divya kalevara</strong><br>
<strong>ananta pratibhā bharā divya guṇadhara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Your divine form’s arms extend to your knees. You possess infinite genius and divine qualities.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaura-kṛṣṇe jāni tava abhinna svarūpa</strong><br>
<strong>saṁsāra tarite ebe śuddha-bhakta-rūpa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I know that you are nondifferent from Śrī Gaura Kṛṣṇa and have now assumed the form of a pure devotee to deliver the world.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rūpānuga-dhārā tumi kara ālokita</strong><br>
<strong>prabhākara sama prabhā bhuvana-vidita</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You illuminate Śrī Rūpa’s line. Like the sun, your effulgence is known throughout the world.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śuddha bhakti prachārite tomā sama nāi</strong><br>
<strong>akalaṅka indu yena dayāla nitāi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">There is no one like you to preach pure devotion. You are a spotless moon like merciful Nityānanda.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ullasita viśva-vāsī labhe prema-dhana</strong><br>
<strong>ānande nāchiyā gāhe tava guṇa-gaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Attaining the wealth of divine love (by your grace), joyful souls all over the world sing your glories, dancing in ecstasy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sthāpilā āśrama bahu jagata mājhāre</strong><br>
<strong>pāramahaṁsa-dharma-jñāna śikṣāra prachāre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You established many ashrams all over the world to teach paramahaṁsa-dharma (the way of the true Vaiṣṇavas).</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chintyāchintya veda-jñāne tumi adhikārī</strong><br>
<strong>sakala saṁśaya chhettvā susiddhānta-dhārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You are a master of both conceivable and inconceivable Vedic knowledge. You cut away all doubts and represent the true teachings.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra mahimā gāhe goloka maṇḍale</strong><br>
<strong>nitya-siddha parikare tava līlā-sthale</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Your eternally liberated associates sing your glories in Goloka, the realm of your Pastimes.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>patita pāvana tumi dayāra samīra</strong><br>
<strong>sarva-kārye sunipuṇa satya-sugambhīra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You are the saviour of the fallen and a breeze of mercy. You are truthful, deep, and expert in all types of service.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>apūrva lekhanī dhārā pragāḍha pāṇḍitya</strong><br>
<strong>sadā hāsya miṣṭa-bhāṣī suśīla kavitva</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Your writings are wonderful, and your learning is profound. You always smile and speak sweet words. Your poetry is perfect.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sādhu-saṅge sadānandī sarala vinayī</strong><br>
<strong>sabhā-madhye vaktā śreṣṭha sarvatra vijayī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You are always joyful in the sādhus’ association. You are simple and humble. In assemblies, you are the best speaker. Everywhere you are victorious.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="14">
<h5>14</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gauḍīya gagane tumi āchārya-bhāskara</strong><br>
<strong>nirantara sevā-priya miṣṭa kaṇṭhasvara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the sky of the Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇavas, you are the sun-like Āchārya. You are always fond of service, and your voice is sweet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="15">
<h5>15</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra karuṇā māge trikāla vilāse</strong><br>
<strong>gāndharvikā-giridhārī sevā-mātra āśe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Throughout the play of the past, present, and future, this servant prays for your mercy, desiring only to serve Śrī Śrī Gāndharvikā-Giridhārī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="16">
<h5>16</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛpā kara ohe prabhu śrī-gaura-prakāśa</strong><br>
<strong>ārati karaye sadā e adhama dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Be merciful, O lord, manifestation of Śrī Gaurāṅga! This fallen servant always worships you.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jaya-guru-maharaja-jati-rajeshvara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Guru Ārati Stuti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya ‘guru-mahārāja’ yati-rājeśvara</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-bhakti-rakṣaka deva-gosvāmī śrīdhara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to Śrīla Guru Mahārāj, Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī, the leading general of the tridaṇḍi-sannyāsīs!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>patita-pāvana-līlā vistāri’ bhuvane</strong><br>
<strong>nistārilā dīna-hīna āpāmara jane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You revealed your Pastimes in this world as the saviour of the fallen and delivered the sinful and unfortunate souls.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra karuṇā-ghana murati heriyā</strong><br>
<strong>preme bhāgyavāna jīva paḍe murachiyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Beholding your intensely merciful form, the fortunate souls faint with divine love.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sudīrgha supībya deha divya-bhāvāśraya</strong><br>
<strong>divya-jñāna-dīpta-netra divya-jyotirmaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Your lofty, delightful form is an abode of divine emotion and filled with divine lustre. Your eyes are illumined with divine knowledge.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>suvarṇa-sūraja-kānti aruṇa-vasana</strong><br>
<strong>tilaka, tulasī-mālā, chandana-bhūṣaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Your form is adorned with tilak, tulasī beads, sandalwood paste, saffron cloth, and golden radiance like that of the sun.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>apūrva śrī-aṅga-śobhā kare jhalamala</strong><br>
<strong>audārya-unnata-bhāva mādhurya-ujjvala</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The extraordinary beauty of your divine form shines with the exalted joy of benevolence, the brightness of divine sweetness.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>achintya pratibhā, snigdha, gambhīra, udāra</strong><br>
<strong>jaḍa-jñāna-giri-vajra divya-dīkṣādhāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You are endowed with inconceivable genius, affection, gravity, and magnanimity. You are a thunderbolt atop the mountain of mundane knowledge, and the conduit for divine revelation.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaura-saṅkīrtana-rāsa-rasera āśraya</strong><br>
<strong>“dayāla nitāi” nāme nitya premamaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You are the abode of the joy of Śrī Gaurāṅga’s saṅkīrtan dance, and you are always filled with divine love as you call “Dayāl Nitāi”.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sāṅgopāṅge gaura-dhāme nitya-parakāśa</strong><br>
<strong>gupta-govardhane divya-līlāra-vilāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The play of your divine Pastimes at Hidden Govardhan Hill in Śrī Gaurāṅga’s abode are manifest eternally.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gauḍīya-āchārya-goṣṭhī-gaurava-bhājana</strong><br>
<strong>gauḍīya-siddhānta-maṇi kaṇṭha-vibhūṣaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You are revered by the assembly of Gauḍīya Āchāryas. Your neck is adorned with the jewel of Gauḍīya-siddhānta.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaura-sarasvatī-sphūrta siddhāntera khani</strong><br>
<strong>āviṣkṛta gāyatrīra artha-chintāmaṇi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You are the mine of the siddhānta revealed by Śrī Gaurāṅga and Śrīla Saraswatī Ṭhākur. You discovered the divine meaning of the gāyatrī mantra.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>eka-tattva varṇanete nitya-nava-bhāva</strong><br>
<strong>susaṅgati, sāmañjasya, e saba prabhāva</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Your ever-new joy as you describe the Absolute, your adjustments, your harmonies—such greatness …</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra satīrtha-varga sabe eka-mate</strong><br>
<strong>rūpa-sarasvatī-dhārā dekhena tomāte</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Unanimously all of your godbrothers see within you the revelation of Śrī Rūpa and Śrīla Saraswatī Ṭhākur.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="14">
<h5>14</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tulasī-mālikā-haste śrī-nāma-grahaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>dekhi’ sakalera haya ‘prabhu’ uddīpana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Seeing you chant the Holy Name with tulasī beads in hand, everyone remembers Prabhupād Śrīla Saraswatī Ṭhākur.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="15">
<h5>15</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>koṭī-chandra-suśītala o pada bharasā</strong><br>
<strong>gāndharvā-govinda-līlāmṛta-lābha-āśā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The shelter of your feet, which are cooling like millions of moons, is our hope to attain the nectar of Śrī Śrī RādhāGovinda’s Pastimes</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="16">
<h5>16</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>avichintya-bhedābheda-siddhānta-prakāśa</strong><br>
<strong>sānande ārati stuti kare dīna-dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O manifestation of the principle of inconceivable difference and nondifference (union in separation)! This humble servant chants your glories and worships you with joy.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-kali-kukkura-kadana-jadi-chao-he" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Maṅgal Ārati <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kali-kukkura-kadana yadi chāo he</strong><br>
<strong>kali-yuga-pāvana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kali-bhaya-nāśana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-śachī-nandana gāo he</strong></p>
<p class="translation">If you want to tame the dog Kali, then chant ‘Śrī Śachī Nandan!’ He is the saviour and destroyer of fear in Kali-yuga.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gadādhara-mādana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nitāi-era prāṇa-dhana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;advaitera prapūjita gorā</strong><br>
<strong>nimāi viśvambhara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrīnivāsa-īśvara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakta-samūha chita-chorā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is Gadādhar’s joy, and Nitāi’s life and wealth. He is worshipped by Advaita Āchārya. He is known as Nimāi, and is the maintainer of the universe. He is Śrīvās Ṭhākur’s Lord, and He steals the devotees’ hearts.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nadīyā-śaśadhara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;māyāpura-īśvara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāma-pravartana-śūra</strong><br>
<strong>gṛhī-jana-śikṣaka&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nyāsi-kula-nāyaka</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mādhava rādhā-bhāva-pūra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is the moon of Nadia, the Lord of Māyāpur, and the inaugurator of Hari-nām-saṅkīrtan. He is the teacher of the householders and the leader of the sannyāsīs. He is Mādhava replete with Rādhā’s heart.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sārvabhauma-śodhana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gajapati-tāraṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rāmānanda-poṣaṇa vīra</strong><br>
<strong>rūpānanda-vardhana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sanātana-pālana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;haridāsa-modana dhīra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is the purifier of Sārvabhauma Bhaṭṭāchārya and the deliverer of Mahārāj Pratāparudra. He is the maintainer of Rāmānanda Rāy and the hero of all. He is the guardian of Śrīla Sanātan Goswāmī and the joy of Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī. He is the delight of Hari Dās Ṭhākur and is perfectly sober.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vraja-rasa-bhāvana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;duṣṭa-mata-śātana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kapaṭī-vighātana-kāma</strong><br>
<strong>śuddha bhakta-pālana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śuṣka-jñāna-tāḍana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chhala-bhakti-dūṣaṇa rāma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is absorbed in Vraja’s rasas. He is the dispeller of misconception, lust, and deceit. He is the maintainer of the pure devotees and the dispeller of dry knowledge. He is the eradicator of pseudo-devotion and the reservoir of all pleasure.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jashomati-nandana-vraja-vara-nagara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Nām-kīrtan <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yaśomatī-nandana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vraja-vara-nāgara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gokula-rañjana kāna</strong><br>
<strong>gopī-parāṇa-dhana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;madana-manohara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kāliya-damana vidhāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Kṛṣṇa, who is known as Kān, is Yaśodā’s darling son, Vraja’s paramour, Gokula’s delight, the gopīs’ life and wealth, the enchanter of Cupid, and the conqueror of Kālīya.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;amala hari-nāma amiya-vilāsā</strong><br>
<strong>vipina-purandara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;navīna nāgara-vara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vaṁśī-vadana suvāsā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">His Names and nectarean Pastimes are all transcendental. He is the king of Vraja’s forests and the best of young paramours. He beautifully plays the flute.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vraja-jana-pālana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;asura-kula-nāśana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nanda-godhana-rākhoyālā</strong><br>
<strong>govinda mādhava&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;navanīta-taṣkara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sundara nanda-gopālā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is the guardian of Vraja’s residents, the destroyer of the demons, and the keeper of Nanda’s cows. He is known as ‘Govinda’—He who nourishes the cows, senses, Vedas, and world—and ‘Mādhava’—Śrī Rādhā’s consort. He is Nanda’s beautiful cowherd boy and a butter thief.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yamunā-taṭa-chara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopī-vasana-hara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rāsa-rasika kṛpāmaya</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-rādhā-vallabha&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vṛndāvana-naṭa-vara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakativinod-āśraya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is a spy on the Yamunā’s banks and the stealer of the gopīs’ garments. He is the relisher of the rāsa-līlā and the embodiment of mercy. He is Śrī Rādhā’s beloved, Vṛndāvan’s great dancer, and Bhakti Vinod’s shelter.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jaya-shiachinandana-sura-muni-vandana" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Śachīnandana-vandanā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya śachī-nandana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sura-muni-vandana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhava-bhaya-khaṇḍana jaya he</strong><br>
<strong>jaya hari-kīrtana-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nartanāvartana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kali-mala-kartana jaya he</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to Śrī Śachī Nandan, who is worshipped by the demigods and sages! All glory to He who destroys all fear of material existence! All glory to the inaugurator of dancing and chanting the Lord’s Names! All glory to He who removes the contamination of Kali!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nayana-purandara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viśvarūpa snehadhara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viśvambhara viśvera kalyāṇa</strong><br>
<strong>jaya lakṣmī-viṣṇu-priyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viśvambhara priya hiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jaya priya kiṅkara īśāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Śachī Nandan is the delight of everyone’s eyes and affectionate to His brother Viśvarūp. He is the maintainer of the world and the embodiment of its good fortune. All glory to Lakṣmī Priyā and Viṣṇu Priyā, who are dear to Viśvambhar’s heart! All glory to His dear servant Īśān!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī sītā advaita rāya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mālinī śrīvāsa jaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jaya chandraśekhara āchārya</strong><br>
<strong>jaya nityānanda rāya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gadādhara jaya jaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jaya haridāsa nāmāchārya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to Sītā Ṭhākurāṇī and Advaita Āchārya! All glory to Mālinī Devī and Śrīvās Ṭhākur! All glory to Chandraśekhar Āchārya! All glory to Nityānanda Rāy! All glory to Gadādhar Paṇḍit! All glory to the Nāmāchārya, Hari Dās Ṭhākur!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>murāri mukunda jaya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;premanidhi mahāśaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jaya yata prabhu pāriṣada</strong><br>
<strong>vandi sabākāra pāya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;adhamere kṛpā haya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakti sapārṣada-prabhupāda</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to Murāri Gupta, Mukunda Datta, and Puṇḍarīk Vidyānidhi! All glory to all the associates of Śrī Śachī Nandan! I offer my obeisance at their feet. By their mercy upon this fallen soul, I serve Śrīla Prabhupād and his associates.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-bhaja-bhakata-vatsala-shri-gaurahari" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Bhoga Ārati Gīti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhaja bhakata-vatsala śrī-gaurahari</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-gaurahari sohi goṣṭha-vihārī</strong><br>
<strong>nanda-yaśomatī-chita-hārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Serve Śrī Gaurahari, who is most affectionate to His devotees. He is the rambler in the fields (of Vraja), the stealer of Nanda and Yaśodā’s hearts.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>“belā ha’lo, dāmodara, āisa ekhana</strong><br>
<strong>bhoga-mandire vasi’ karaha bhojana”</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“It’s time! Damodar! Come now! Sit down in the dining hall and eat.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nandera nirdeśe vaise giri-vara-dhārī</strong><br>
<strong>baladeva-saha sakhā vaise sāri sāri</strong></p>
<p class="translation">At Nanda’s request, Kṛṣṇa, Baladev, and Their friends sit down in lines.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śuktā-śākādi bhāji nālitā kuṣmāṇḍa</strong><br>
<strong>ḍāli ḍālnā dugdha-tumbī dadhi mochā-ghaṇṭa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śuktā, śāk, fried vegetables, jute-leaf salad, pumpkin, baskets of fruit, dāl patties, gourd cooked in milk, yoghurt, banana flower …</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mudga-baḍā māṣa-baḍā roṭikā ghṛtānna</strong><br>
<strong>śaṣkulī piṣṭaka kṣīr puli pāyasānna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Moong dāl patties, urad dāl patties, chapātīs, rice with ghee, rice and sesame gruel, rice-flour cakes, condensed milk, pancakes, rice pudding …</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>karpūra amṛta-kelī rambhā kṣīra-sāra</strong><br>
<strong>amṛta rasālā, amla dvādaśa prakāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Hot milk flavoured with camphor, plantains, butter, twelve kinds of sweet, juicy, and sour preparations …</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>luchi chini sarapurī lāḍḍu rasāvalī</strong><br>
<strong>bhojana karena kṛṣṇa ha’ye kutūhalī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Sweet purīs, purīs filled with cream, sweet balls, dāl patties boiled with sugared rice—Kṛṣṇa eagerly eats all these preparations.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhikāra pakka anna vividha vyañjana</strong><br>
<strong>parama ānande kṛṣṇa karena bhojana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">With great joy Kṛṣṇa eats various vegetable preparations cooked by Rādhikā.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chhale-bale lāḍḍu khāy śrī-madhumaṅgala</strong><br>
<strong>bagala bājāy āra deya haribolo</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By hook or by crook, Madhumaṅgal eats the sweet balls. He slaps his armpits and calls out, ‘Haribol!’</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhikādi gaṇe heri’ nayanera koṇe</strong><br>
<strong>tṛpta ha’ye khāya kṛṣṇa yaśodā-bhavane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Glancing at Rādhikā and Her friends out of the corners of His eyes, Kṛṣṇa happily eats in Yaśodā’s home.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhojanānte piye kṛṣṇa suvāsita vāri</strong><br>
<strong>sabe mukha prakṣālaya ha’ye sāri sāri</strong></p>
<p class="translation">After eating, Kṛṣṇa drinks scented water. Then all of the boys line up and wash their mouths.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hasta-mukha prakṣāliyā yata sakhā-gaṇe</strong><br>
<strong>ānande viśrāma kare baladeva-sane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">After washing their hands and mouths, Kṛṣṇa’s friends happily take rest with Baladev.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jambula rasāla āne tāmbula-maśālā</strong><br>
<strong>tāhā kheye kṛṣṇachandra sukhe nidrā gelā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Jambula and Rasāla bring spiced pān. Kṛṣṇa chews it and then happily goes to sleep.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="14">
<h5>14</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>viśālākṣa śikhi-puchchha chāmara ḍhulāya</strong><br>
<strong>apūrva śayāya kṛṣṇa sukhe nidrā yāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Viśālākṣa fans Kṛṣṇa with a peacock fan, and Kṛṣṇa happily sleeps on a comfortable bed.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="15">
<h5>15</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yaśomatī-ājñā peye dhaniṣṭhā ānīta</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-kṛṣṇa-prasāda rādhā bhuñje haye prīta</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Being requested by Yaśodā, Dhaniṣṭhā brings Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s remnants to Rādhā, who eats them joyfully.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="16">
<h5>16</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>lalitādi sakhī-gaṇa avaśeṣa pāya</strong><br>
<strong>mane mane sukhe rādhā-kṛṣṇa-guṇa gāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lalitā Devī and her friends then receive Rādhā’s remnants and joyfully sing the glories of Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa within their hearts.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="17">
<h5>17</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari-līlā eka-mātra yāhāra pramoda</strong><br>
<strong>bhogārati gāy ṭhākur bhakativinoda</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Ṭhākur Bhakti Vinod, whose sole joy is the Lord’s Pastimes, sings this bhog ārati.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>(bhaja govinda govinda gopāla)</strong><br>
<strong>(giridhārī gopīnātha nanda-dulāla)</strong><br>
<strong>(bhaja govinda govinda gopāla)</strong><br>
<strong>(nanda-dulāla jaya jaya śachī-dulāla)</strong><br>
<strong>(śachī-dulāla jaya jaya gaura gopāla)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Serve Govinda, serve Gopāl. Serve Giridhārī, Gopīnāth, Nanda Dulāl. Serve Govinda, serve Gopāl. All glory to Nanda’s son! All glory to Śachī’s son! All glory to golden Gopāl!</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jaya-jaya-gaurachander-arotiko-shobha" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Gaura Ārati <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>(kibā) jaya jaya gorāchā̐der āratika śobhā</strong><br>
<strong>jāhnavī-taṭa-vane jaga-mana lobhā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(jaga-janer mana lobhā)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(gaurāṅger ārati śobhā jaga-janer mana lobhā)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(nitāi gaura haribol)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to the beautiful ārati to Śrī Gorāchā̐d in a grove on the banks of the Ganges, which attracts the hearts of all souls throughout the world!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>(kibā) dakṣiṇe nitāichā̐da bāme gadādhara</strong><br>
<strong>nikaṭe advaita śrīnivāsa chhatra-dhara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">On Gorāchā̐d’s right is Nitāichā̐d, and on His left is Gadādhar. Advaita is nearby, and Śrīvās holds an umbrella.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>(kibā) basiyāchhe gorāchā̐da ratna-siṁhāsane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ratna-simhāsana alo karere</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;basiyāchhen gorāchā̐d ratna-siṁhāsane)</strong><br>
<strong>ārati karena brahmā ādi-deva gaṇe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(gaurāṅger ārati kare)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(brahmā ādi-deva gaṇe gaurāṅger ārati kare)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(gaurāṅgera bhakta sane gaurāṅger ārati kare)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(nitāi gaura haribol)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Gorāchā̐d has sat on a jewelled throne. Brahmā and the other demigods worship Him (in the company of His devotees).</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>(kibā) narahari-ādi kari chāmara ḍhulāya</strong><br>
<strong>sañjaya-mukunda-vāsu-ghoṣa-ādi gāya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(gaura guṇa gāna kare)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(sañjaya mukunda vāsu-ghoṣa gaura guṇa gāna kare)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(nitāi gaura haribol)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Narahari and others fan the Lord with chāmaras. Sañjay, Mukunda, Vāsudev Ghoṣ, and others sing.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>(kibā) śaṅkha bāje ghaṇṭā bāje bāje karatāla</strong><br>
<strong>madhura mṛdaṅga bāje parama rasāla</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(madhur madhur madhur bāje)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(gaurāṅger ārati kāle madhur madhur madhur bāje)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(śaṅkha bāje ghaṇṭā bāje madhur madhur madhur bāje)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(madhura mṛdanga bāje madhur madhur madhur bāje)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(nitāi gaura haribol)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Conches, bells, kartāls, and mṛdaṅgas resound sweetly. The kīrtan is most relishable.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>(kibā) bahu-koṭi chandra jini vadana ujjvala</strong><br>
<strong>gala-deśe vana-mālā kare jhalamala</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(jhalmal jhalmal jhalmal kare)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(gaura gale vana phuler mālā jhalmal jhalmal jhalmal kare)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(nitāi gaura haribol)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The radiance of the Lord’s face surpasses that of millions of moons. The garland of forest flowers around His neck shines.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>(kibā) śiva-śuka-nārada preme gada-gada</strong><br>
<strong>bhakati-vinoda dekhe gorāra sampada</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śiva, Śukadev, and Nārad stammer in the ecstasy of love. In this way Bhakti Vinod beholds Gorāchā̐d’s glory.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>(ei bār āmāy dayā kara)</strong><br>
<strong>(ṭhākura śrī bhakti-vinoda ei bār āmāy dayā kara)</strong><br>
<strong>(ṭhākura śrī sarasvatī ei bār āmāy dayā kara)</strong><br>
<strong>(patit-pāvan śrī gurudev ei bār āmāy dayā kara)</strong><br>
<strong>(sapārṣada gaurahari ei bār āmāy dayā kara)</strong><br>
<strong>(nitāi gaura haribol)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">This time please give me your grace. O Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur, O Śrīla Saraswatī Ṭhākur, O Gurudev, saviour of the fallen, O Gaurahari, along with your associates, this time please give me your grace.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jayare-jayare-jaya-gaura-sarasvati" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Sāraswat Ārati <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jayare jayare jaya gaura-sarasvatī</strong><br>
<strong>bhakativinodānvaya karuṇā murati</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to the voice of Śrī Gaurāṅga, the embodiment of Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur’s mercy, Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Ṭhākur!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>prakāśile gaura-sevā bhūvana maṅgala</strong><br>
<strong>bhakati-siddhānta śuddha prajñāna ujjvala</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He manifested Śrī Gaurāṅga’s service for the upliftment of the world. His teaching of devotion is pure, brilliant, and enlightening.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhā-śyāma eka-tanu dakṣe gorā rāya</strong><br>
<strong>bāme rādha madhye svayaṁ śyāma-gopa jaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to the combined form of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Śyām, Śrī Gaura Rāy, on the right, Śrī Rādhā on the left, and the dark cowherd boy Himself in the middle!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vraja-rasa nava-bhāve navadvīpe rāje</strong><br>
<strong>udāre madhura rāga abhinava sāje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Vraja’s rasa manifests in a new way in Nabadwīp: madhurarasa dresses newly in benevolence.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mādhurya kaivalya rāga vrajera niryāsa</strong><br>
<strong>prāpti parākāṣṭhā tāhe gaurāṅga vilāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Sweet divine love, Vraja’s essence, reaches its climax in Śrī Gaurāṅga’s Pastimes.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhā bhāva-kānti aṅgikari’ bhāla mate</strong><br>
<strong>dakṣiṇe āsana rasa garimā dekhāte</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Gaurāṅga earnestly embraces Śrī Rādhā’s heart and halo, and takes His place on the right to show His rasa’s glory.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhā-rasa-traya-svāda rahasya prayāsa</strong><br>
<strong>nirakhi praphulla rādhā mukhe manda hāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Seeing Kṛṣṇa’s secret endeavour to taste Her threefold rasa, Rādhā is fulfilled and smiles gently.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>madhye rahi’ vaṁśī-rave ghoṣe vaṁśī-dhara</strong><br>
<strong>rādhāra sampade āmi gaurāṅga-sundara!</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Remaining in the middle, the flute player declares with the song of His flute, “With Rādhā’s wealth, I am Gaurāṅgasundar!”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mad abhīṣṭha rūpa rādhāra hṛdaya mandire</strong><br>
<strong>gaurāṅga bhajile suṣṭhu sphūrti pāya tāre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“If you serve Gaurāṅga, My form which is cherished in the temple of Rādhā’s heart will be fully revealed to you.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nadīyā prakāśe mahāprabhu gaura-nidhi</strong><br>
<strong>patita-pāvana deve milāila vidhi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Gaurāṅga Mahāprabhu appeared in Nadia, and Providence combined the saviour of the fallen (the Kali-yuga-avatār) within Him.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>e-rūpa ārati brahmā śambhu agochara</strong><br>
<strong>gaura-bhakta kṛpā-pātra mātra siddhi sāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">This type of ārati is unknown to Brahmā and Śiva. Only a recipient of Śrī Gaurāṅga’s devotees’ mercy reaches such perfection.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī svarūpa, rāmānanda, rūpa, sanātana</strong><br>
<strong>śrī raghu, jīvādi kṛpāya dekhe bhakta-jana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Devotees see this ārati by the mercy of Śrī Svarūp Dāmodar, Śrī Rāmananda Rāy, Śrī Rūpa, Śrī Sanātan, Śrī Raghunāth, Śrī Jīva, and their associates.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya guru-gaura-rādhā-govinda-sundara</strong><br>
<strong>jaya dāo bhakta vṛnda nitya nirantara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to Śrī Guru, Gaurāṅga, and Rādhā-Govindasundar! O devotees, glorify them always and forever!</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section class="category">
<h1>Parikramā</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-gurudeva-kripa-bindu-diya-kara-ei-dase" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Gurudeva <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gurudeva!</strong><br>
<strong>kṛpā-bindu diyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kara’ ei dāse</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tṛṇāpekṣā ati dīna</strong><br>
<strong>sakala-sahane&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bala diyā kara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nija-māne spṛhā-hīna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gurudev! Give this servant a drop of your mercy, and make me more humble than a blade of grass. Give me the strength to tolerate everything, and make me free from any desire for personal honour.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sakale sammāna&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;karite śakati</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;deha nātha! yathā yatha</strong><br>
<strong>tabe ta’ gāiba&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hari-nāma sukhe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;aparādha habe hata</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O master! Give me the ability to befittingly honour everyone. Then I will sing the Lord’s Name happily, and all my offences will be eradicated.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kabe hena kṛpā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;labhiyā e jana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛtārtha ha-ibe, nātha!</strong><br>
<strong>śakti-buddhi-hīna&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āmi ati dīna</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kara more ātma-sātha</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will this soul attain such mercy and become fulfilled? O master! I am devoid of ability and intelligence, and greatly distressed. Please accept me.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yogyatā-vichāre&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kichhu nāhi pāi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra karuṇā-sāra</strong><br>
<strong>karuṇā nā ha-ile&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kā̐diyā kā̐diyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prāṇa nā rākhiba āra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When I examine myself for qualifications, I find none. Your mercy is everything. If you are not merciful to me, then, crying, I will no longer maintain my life.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-udilo-aruna-puraba-bhage" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Aruṇodoya-kīrtan <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Part 1</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>udila aruṇa pūrava-bhāge</strong><br>
<strong>dvija-maṇi gorā amani jāge</strong><br>
<strong>bhakata-samūha la-iyā sāthe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(gorā) gelā nagara-vrāje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The sun arises in the east, and the jewel of the brāhmaṇs, Śrī Gorāchā̐d, awakes. Taking His devotees with Him, He goes out about the town.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>‘tāthai tāthai’ bājala khola</strong><br>
<strong>ghana ghana tāhe jhā̐jera rola</strong><br>
<strong>preme ḍhala ḍhala sonāra aṅga</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(gorār) charaṇe nūpura bāje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The mṛdaṅgas resound ‘tāthai tāthai’ to the beat of the cymbals as Gaurasundar’s golden form sways with ecstatic love and His footbells jingle.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mukunda mādhava yādava hari</strong><br>
<strong>balena bala re vadana bhari’</strong><br>
<strong>michhe nida-vaśe gela re rāti</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;divasa śarīra sāje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He calls out, “Chant aloud Mukunda, Mādhava, Yādava, Hari! Uselessly you pass the night asleep and the day decorating your body.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>emana durlabha mānava-deha</strong><br>
<strong>pāiyā ki kara bhāvanā keha</strong><br>
<strong>ebe nā bhajile yaśodā-suta</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;charame paḍibe lāje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“Having attained this rare human body, what are you thinking? If you do not serve Kṛṣṇa now, lastly you will fall into shame.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>udita tapana ha-ile asta</strong><br>
<strong>dina gela bali’ ha-ibe vyasta</strong><br>
<strong>tabe kena ebe alasa ha-i’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā bhaja hṛdaya-rāje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“When the shining sun sets, you become busy, considering that the day has passed. So why are you being lazy now? Why don’t you serve the Lord of your heart?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jīvana anitya jānaha sāra</strong><br>
<strong>tāhe nānā-vidha vipada-bhāra</strong><br>
<strong>nāmāśraya kari’ yatane tumi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;thākaha āpana kāje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“Understand the essence: life is temporary and filled with all sorts of adversity. Carefully taking shelter of the Name, engage in your work.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛṣṇa-nāma-sudhā kariyā pāna</strong><br>
<strong>juḍāo bhakati-vinoda-prāṇa</strong><br>
<strong>nāma vinā kichhu nāhika āra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chaudda-bhuvana-mājhe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Drink the nectar of Kṛṣṇa’s Name and soothe Bhakti Vinod’s soul. There is nothing to be had but the Name in all the fourteen worlds.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jīvera kalyāṇa-sādhana-kāma</strong><br>
<strong>jagate āsi’ e madhura nāma</strong><br>
<strong>avidyā-timira-tapana-rūpe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hṛd-gagane virāje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Desiring to bless all souls, the Lord’s sweet Name comes to this world and arises like the Sun within the sky of the heart to dispel the darkness of ignorance.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jiv-jago-jiv-jago-gaurachanda-bole" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Aruṇodoya-kīrtan <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Part 2</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jīva jāga, jīva jāga, gorāchā̐da bale</strong><br>
<strong>kata nidrā yāo māyā-piśāchīra kole</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Gorāchā̐d calls, “O souls, awake! O souls, awake! How long will you sleep in the clutches of the witch Māyā?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>(ār kata kāl ghumāibe?)</strong><br>
<strong>(māyā-piśāchīra kole ār kata kāl ghumāibe?)</strong><br>
<strong>(uṭha jīva, kṛṣṇa bhaja ār kata kāl ghumāibe?)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">"How much longer will you sleep? How much longer will you sleep in the lap of the witch Māyā? Arise soul! Serve Kṛṣṇa! How much longer will you sleep?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhajiba baliyā ese saṁsāra-bhitare</strong><br>
<strong>bhuliyā rahile tumi avidyāra bhare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“You came into this world saying, ‘O Lord, I will serve You’, but, having forgotten this promise, you have remained in ignorance.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>(bhuliyā rahile)</strong><br>
<strong>(avidyāra mohe paḍe tumi bhuliyā rahile)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“You forgot and remained. You fell into illusion’s bewilderment, forgot (your promise), and remained there.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāre la-ite āmi ha-inu avatāra</strong><br>
<strong>āmi vinā bandhu āra ke āchhe tomāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“I have descended to save you. Other than Me, who is your friend?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>(eman bandhu ār ke āchhe?)</strong><br>
<strong>(māyā-ha-ite uddhārite eman bandhu ār ke āchhe?)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“Who else is such a friend? Who else is there to rescue you from Māyā?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>enechhi auṣadhi māyā nāśibāra lāgi’</strong><br>
<strong>hari-nāma mahā-mantra lao tumi māgi’</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“I have brought the medicine to dispel Māyā. Pray for this Hari-nām mahāmantra and take it.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>(māgiyā lao re)</strong><br>
<strong>(sudhā-mākhā ei hari-nām māgiyā lao re)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“Pray and take it. Pray for the Holy Name, which is dripping with nectar, and take it.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakativinoda prabhura-charaṇe paḍiyā</strong><br>
<strong>sei hari-nāma-mantra la-ila māgiyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bhakti Vinod falls at the Lord’s feet, prays for the Hari-nām mahāmantra, and takes it.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-kobe-shri-chaitanya-more-koribena-daya" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Dainya Prārthanā Gīti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kabe śrī chaitanya more karibena dayā</strong><br>
<strong>kabe āmi pāiba vaiṣṇava-pada-chhāyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will Śrī Chaitanya be merciful to me? When will I reach the shade of the Vaiṣṇavas’ feet?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kabe āmi chhāḍiba e viṣayābhimāna</strong><br>
<strong>kabe viṣṇu-jane āmi kariba sammāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will I give up this mundane ego? When will I honour the Lord’s devotees?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gala-vastra kṛtāñjali vaiṣṇava-nikaṭe</strong><br>
<strong>dante tṛṇa kari’ dā̐ḍāiba niṣkapaṭe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">With a cloth around my neck, joined palms, and straw between my teeth, I will sincerely submit myself at the Vaiṣṇava’s feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kā̐diyā kā̐diyā jānāiba duḥkha-grāma</strong><br>
<strong>saṁsāra-anala haite māgiba viśrāma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Weeping, I will convey my sorrows to the Vaiṣṇava and pray for shelter from the fire of material existence.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śuniyā āmāra duḥkha vaiṣṇava ṭhākura</strong><br>
<strong>āmā’ lāgi’ kṛṣṇe āvedibena prachura</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Hearing of my sorrow, the worshippable Vaiṣṇava will thoroughly pray to Kṛṣṇa on my behalf.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vaiṣṇavera āvedane kṛṣṇa dayāmaya</strong><br>
<strong>e hena pāmara prati habena sadaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Satisfied by the Vaiṣṇava’s prayer, Kṛṣṇa will be merciful to this sinner.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vinodera nivedana vaiṣṇava-charaṇe</strong><br>
<strong>kṛpā kari’ saṅge laha ei akiñchane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bhakti Vinod prays at the feet of the Vaiṣṇavas: “Mercifully take this lowly soul with you.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-namo-namah-tulasi-maharani" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Tulasī-parikramā-gīti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Chandraśekhar Āchārya</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>namo namaḥ tulasī mahārāṇī</strong><br>
<strong>vṛnde mahārāṇī namo namaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Tulasī Mahārāṇī, O Vṛndā Devī, I offer my obeisance unto you again and again.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>namo re namo re mā̐iyā namo nārāyaṇī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Nārāyaṇī, I offer my obeisance unto you again and again.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yā̐ko daraśe paraśe agha-nāśa hoi</strong><br>
<strong>mahimā veda-purāṇe vākhāni</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By seeing or touching you, all sins are destroyed. Your glories are sung in the Vedas and Purāṇas.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yā̐ko patra mañjarī komala</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-pati-charaṇa-kamale lepaṭāni</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Your leaves and soft mañjarīs are twined around the lotus feet of Lakṣmī’s Lord.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dhanya tulasī pūraṇa tapa kiye</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-śālagrāma-mahā-pāṭarāṇī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O fortunate Tulasī, by your performance of austerity you have become Śrī Śālagrām’s worshippable consort.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dhūpa, dīpa, naivedya, ārati</strong><br>
<strong>phulanā kiye varakhā varakhāni</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You shower mercy upon one who offers you incense, lamps, foods, worship, and flowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chhāppānna bhoga, chhatriśa vyañjana</strong><br>
<strong>vinā tulasī prabhu eka nāhi māni</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The Lord does not accept even one of fifty-six grain offerings or thirty-six vegetable offerings without tulasī leaves.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śiva śuka nārada ā-ura brahmādika</strong><br>
<strong>ḍhu̐ḍata phirata mahā-muni jñānī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śiva, Śukadev, Nārad, Brahmā, the other demigods, and the great learned sages all circumambulate you.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chandraśekhara māiyā, terā yaśa gāoye</strong><br>
<strong>bhakati dāna dījiye mahārāṇī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Tulasī Mahārāṇī, Chandraśekhar sings your glories. Give him the gift of devotion.</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section class="category">
<h1>Vandanā</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-vande-ham-shri-guroh-shri-yuta-pada-kamalam" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Vandanā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="verse"><strong>vande ’haṁ śrī-guroḥ śrī-yuta-pada-kamalaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-gurūn vaiṣṇavāṁś cha</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-rūpaṁ sāgrajātaṁ saha-gaṇa raghunāth-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ānvitaṁ taṁ sa-jīvam</strong><br>
<strong>sādvaitaṁ sāvadhūtaṁ parijana-sahitaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa-chaitanya-devaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-rādhā-kṛṣṇa-pādān saha-gaṇa-lalitā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-viśākhānvitāṁś cha</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my obeisance unto the lotus feet of my Guru, the predecessor Gurus, the Lord’s devotees, Śrī Rūpa, Śrī Sanātan, Śrī Raghunāth, Śrī Jīva, Śrī Rūpa’s followers, Śrī Kṛṣṇa Chaitanyadev, Śrī Nityānanda Prabhu, Śrī Advaita Prabhu, Śrī Chaitanyadev’s associates, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa, Śrī Lalitā Devī, Śrī Viśākhā Devī, and all of Their associates.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā</strong><br>
<strong>chakṣur unmilitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my obeisance unto Śrī Gurudev, who has opened my eyes, which were blinded by the darkness of ignorance, with the salve of divine knowledge.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>pūjya śrī-guru-varga-vandita mahābhāvānvitāyāḥ sadā</strong><br>
<strong>paurvā parya paramparā prachalita prājya pramūrttā kṛteḥ</strong><br>
<strong>bhakter nirmala-nirjharasya nibhṛtaṁ saṁrakṣakaṁ sādaraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vande śrī-gurudevam ānata-śirā āchārya-varyaṁ nijam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I bow my head in eternal obeisance to my Gurudev, the best of Āchāryas, Śrīla Bhakti Nirmal Āchārya Mahārāj. He is the ever vigilant, stalwart guardian of the current of pure devotion whose highest form flows from our most worshippable Śrī Rūpānuga Guru-varga in their exclusive dedication to Mahābhāva, Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>gurvvābhīṣṭa-supūrakaṁ guru-gaṇair āśīṣa-saṁbhūṣitaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>chintyāchintya-samasta-veda-nipuṇaṁ śrī-rūpa-panthānugam</strong><br>
<strong>govindābhidham ujjvalaṁ vara-tanuṁ bhakty anvitaṁ-sundaraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vande viśva-guruñ cha divya-bhagavat-premṇo hi bīja-pradam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my obeisance unto he who perfectly fulfils his Gurudev’s most cherished desires; who is fully adorned with the blessings of his Gurudev’s associates; who is expert in all aspects of Vedic knowledge, both conceivable and inconceivable; who is the pre-eminent follower of Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī Prabhu’s line; who is known as ‘Govinda’— Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj; whose beautiful, effulgent, exalted form is infused with pure devotion; who is the Guru of the entire universe; and who is the giver of the seed of divine love for the Supreme Lord.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>devaṁ divya-tanuṁ suchanda-vadanaṁ bālārka-chelāñchitaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>sāndrānanda-puraṁ sad-eka-varaṇaṁ vairāgya-vidyāmbudhim</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-siddhānta-nidhiṁ subhakti-lasitaṁ sārasvatānām varaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vande taṁ śubhadaṁ mad-eka-śaraṇaṁ nyāsīśvaraṁ śrīdharam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my obeisance unto my lord, Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj, whose speech is most pleasing; whose divine form is adorned with garments the colour of the newly-risen sun; who is an abode of intense, ecstatic devotion; who is the sādhus’ sole choice; who is an ocean of renunciation and knowledge; who is a treasure-house of perfect conclusions; who is resplendent with pure devotion; who is the best of Śrīla Saraswatī Ṭhākur’s followers; who is the giver of all good; who is the leading general of the tridaṇḍi-sannyāsīs; and who is my sole shelter.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-siddhānta-sarasvatīti vidito gauḍīya-gurv-anvaye</strong><br>
<strong>bhāto bhānuriva prabhāta-gagane yo gaura-saṅkīrtanaiḥ</strong><br>
<strong>māyāvāda-timiṅgilodara-gatān uddhṛtya jīvanimān</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-prema-sudhābdhi-gāhana-sukhaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prādāt prabhuṁ taṁ bhaje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I serve my lord, who is known as Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Ṭhākur. Within the Gauḍīya sampradāya’s lineage of Gurus, he shines like the sun in the morning sky. Through the saṅkīrtan of Śrīman Mahāprabhu he rescues the souls engulfed within the belly of the timiṅgila of illusionism and bestows upon them the ecstasy of swimming in the nectarean ocean of divine love for Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>namo gaurakiśorāya</strong><br>
<strong>bhaktāvadhūta mūrtaye</strong><br>
<strong>gaurāṅghri padma-bhṛṅgāya</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-bhāva-niṣeviṇe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my obeisance unto the divine form of the devoteeavadhūt Śrīla Gaura Kiśor Dās Bābājī Mahārāj, who is a honey-bee at Śrī Gaurasundar’s lotus feet and a servant of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī imbued with deep loving attachment.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>vande bhaktivinodaṁ śrī-</strong><br>
<strong>gaura-śakti-svarūpakam</strong><br>
<strong>bhakti-śāstrajña-samrājaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-rasa-sudhā-nidhim</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my obeisance unto Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur, the embodiment of Śrī Gaurasundar’s potency. He is the king of all knowers of the scriptures of pure devotion and an ocean of the nectar of devotion to Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>gaura-vrajāśritāśeṣair</strong><br>
<strong>vaiṣṇavair vandya-vigraham</strong><br>
<strong>jagannātha-prabhuṁ vande</strong><br>
<strong>premābdhiṁ vṛddha-vaiṣṇavam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my obeisance unto my lord, the ocean of divine love, Śrīla Jagannāth Dās Bābājī Mahārāj. He is the senior-most devotee, and worshipped by all who have taken shelter in Śrī Nabadwīp Dhām and Śrī Vraja Dhām.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>vāñchhā-kalpatarubhyaś cha</strong><br>
<strong>kṛpā-sindhubhya eva cha</strong><br>
<strong>patitānāṁ pāvanebhyo</strong><br>
<strong>vaiṣṇavebhyo namo namaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Again and again I offer my obeisance unto the Supreme Lord’s devotees, who are saviours of the fallen, oceans of mercy, and wish-fulfilling trees.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>pañcha-tattvātmakaṁ kṛṣṇaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhakta-rūpa-svarūpakam</strong><br>
<strong>bhaktāvatāraṁ bhaktākhyaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>namāmi bhakta-śaktikam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my obeisance unto the five-fold manifestation of Śrī Kṛṣṇa known as the Pañcha-Tattva: His form as a devotee (Śrī Kṛṣṇa Chaitanya), His expanded form as a devotee (Śrī Nityānanda Prabhu), His Avatār as a devotee (Śrī Advaita Prabhu), His pure devotees (led by Śrīvās Ṭhākur), and His devotional energies (led by Śrī Gadādhar Paṇḍit).</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>namo mahā-vadānyāya kṛṣṇa-prema-pradāya te</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇāya kṛṣṇa-chaitanya-nāmne gaura-tviṣe namaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Again and again I offer my obeisance unto You, Śrī Kṛṣṇa Chaitanya, the supremely merciful, golden form of Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the giver of Kṛṣṇa-prema.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>jayatāṁ suratau paṅgor mama manda-mater gatī</strong><br>
<strong>mat-sarvasva-padāmbhojau rādhā-madana-mohanau</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to the merciful Śrī Śrī Rādhā–Madan Mohan! Though I am lame and wicked, They are my sole shelter, and Their lotus feet are everything to me.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>dīvyad-vṛndāraṇya-kalpa-drumādhaḥ-</strong><br>
<strong>śrīmad-ratnāgāra-siṁhāsana-sthau</strong><br>
<strong>śrī gāndharvā-śrīla-govinda-devau</strong><br>
<strong>preṣṭhālībhiḥ sevyamānau smarāmi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I meditate upon Śrī Rādhā and Śrī Govinda being worshipped by Their dear girlfriends as They sit upon a throne within a beautiful jeweled temple beneath the wish-fulfilling trees of the divine abode of Vṛndāvan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>śrīmān rāsa-rasārambhī vaṁśī-vaṭa-taṭa-sthitaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>karṣan veṇu-svanair gopīr gopīnāthaḥ śriye ’stu naḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">May Gopīnāth, the beautiful originator of the rāsa-līlā, who stands on the riverbank at Vaṁśī Vaṭ and attracts the gopīs with His flute-song, bestow auspiciousness upon us.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>vṛndāyai tulasī-devyai priyāyai keśavasya cha</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-bhakti-prade devi satyavatyai namo namaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Again and again I offer my obeisance unto Vṛndā Devī, who is known as Tulasī Devī and Satyavatī, who is most dear to Kṛṣṇa, and who bestows devotion to Kṛṣṇa upon us.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>atha natvā mantra-gurūn gurūn bhāgavatārthadān</strong><br>
<strong>vyāsān jagad-gurūn natvā tato jayam udīrayet</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Having offered obeisance to one’s initiating Guru, to the Gurus who have taught one the meaning of the Bhāgavat, and to all the authors of Vedic literature, who are the Gurus of the entire world, proclaim “Jay!”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>jayaḥ sa-parikara śrī-śrī-guru-gaurāṅga-gāndharvā-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;govindasundar-pādapadmānāṁ jayastu!</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to the lotus feet of Śrī Guru, Śrī Gaurasundar, Śrī Śrī Gāndharvā-Govindasundar, and all of Their associates!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-kṛṣṇa-chaitanya prabhu nityānanda</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-advaita gadādhara śrīvāsādi gaura-bhakta-vṛnda</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>hare kṛṣṇa hare kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa hare hare</strong><br>
<strong>hare rāma hare rāma rāma rāma hare hare</strong></p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section class="category">
<h1>Morning</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-samsara-davanala-lidha-loka" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Gurvāṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Viśvanāth Chakravartī Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>saṁsāra-dāvānala-līḍha-loka</strong><br>
<strong>trāṇāya kāruṇya-ghanāghanatvam</strong><br>
<strong>prāptasya kalyāṇa-guṇārṇavasya</strong><br>
<strong>vande guroḥ śrī-charaṇāravindam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my obeisance unto the lotus feet of Śrī Guru, the ocean of goodness who assumes the form of a cloud of mercy to deliver the souls burning in the blazing fire of material existence.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mahāprabhoḥ kīrtana-nṛtya-gīta-</strong><br>
<strong>vāditra-mādyan-manaso rasena</strong><br>
<strong>romāñcha-kampāśru-taraṅga-bhājo</strong><br>
<strong>vande guroḥ śrī-charaṇāravindam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my obeisance unto the lotus feet Śrī Guru, who sheds waves of tears, trembles, and experiences horripilation through his devotion to Śrīman Mahāprabhu and becomes maddened at heart by chanting, dancing, singing, and playing instruments.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-vigrahārādhana-nitya-nānā</strong><br>
<strong>śṛṅgāra-tan-mandira-mārjanādau</strong><br>
<strong>yuktasya bhaktāṁś cha niyuñjato ’pi</strong><br>
<strong>vande guroḥ śrī-charaṇāravindam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my obeisance unto the lotus feet of Śrī Guru, who daily engages himself and other devotees in dressing the Deity with various ornaments, cleaning the Deity’s temple, and performing other devotional practices.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chatur-vidha-śrī-bhagavat-prasāda</strong><br>
<strong>svādv-anna-tṛptān hari-bhakta-saṅghān</strong><br>
<strong>kṛtvaiva tṛptiṁ bhajataḥ sadaiva</strong><br>
<strong>vande guroḥ śrī-charaṇāravindam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my obeisance unto the lotus feet of Śrī Guru, who is always satisfied by satisfying the Lord’s devotees with the four types of tasteful Bhagavat-prasād.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-rādhikā-mādhavayor apāra-</strong><br>
<strong>mādhurya-līlā-guṇa-rūpa-nāmnām</strong><br>
<strong>prati-kṣaṇāsvādana-lolupasya</strong><br>
<strong>vande guroḥ śrī-charaṇāravindam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my obeisance unto the lotus feet of Śrī Guru, who is always eager to relish the unlimitedly sweet Names, Forms, Qualities, and Pastimes of Śrī Śrī Rādhikā-Mādhava.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nikuñja-yūno rati-keli-siddhyair</strong><br>
<strong>yā yālibhir yuktir apekṣaṇīyā</strong><br>
<strong>tatrāti-dākṣyād ati-vallabhasya</strong><br>
<strong>vande guroḥ śrī-charaṇāravindam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my obeisance unto the lotus feet of Śrī Guru, who is very expert in the arts performed by the sakhīs to fulfil the Forest Couple’s Love Play, and is thus very dear to Them.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sākṣād-dharitvena samasta-śāstrair</strong><br>
<strong>uktas tathā bhāvyata eva sadbhiḥ</strong><br>
<strong>kintu prabhor yaḥ priya eva tasya</strong><br>
<strong>vande guroḥ śrī-charaṇāravindam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my obeisance unto the lotus feet of Śrī Guru, who is said by all the scriptures to be the Lord Himself, and considered to be so by the sādhus, yet is the Lord’s beloved devotee.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo</strong><br>
<strong>yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto ’pi</strong><br>
<strong>dhyāyaṁ stuvaṁs tasya yaśas tri-sandhyaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vande guroḥ śrī-charaṇaravindam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my obeisance unto the lotus feet of Śrī Guru, praising and meditating upon his glory at the three junctions of the day. By his grace, one gains the Lord’s grace. Without his grace, one has no shelter anywhere.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrīmad guroraṣṭakam etad uchchair</strong><br>
<strong>brāhme muhūrte paṭhati prayātnāt</strong><br>
<strong>yas tena vṛndāvana-nātha-sākṣāt</strong><br>
<strong>sevaiva labhyā januṣo ’nta eva</strong></p>
<p class="translation">One who very carefully recites aloud this Śrī Gurvaṣṭakam during the brāhma-muhūrta (the ninety-six minute period before sunrise) attains direct service to the Lord of Vṛndāvan at the end of life.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shri-krishna-chaitanya-prabhu-jive-daya-kori" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Auspicious Invocation to Śaraṇāgati <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-kṛṣṇa-chaitanya prabhu jīve dayā kari’</strong><br>
<strong>svapārṣada svīya dhāma saha avatari</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Being merciful to the souls, Śrī Kṛṣṇa Chaitanya Mahāprabhu descends with His associates and abode.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>atyanta durlabha prema karibāre dāna</strong><br>
<strong>śikhāya śaraṇāgati bhakatera prāṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">To distribute the most rare form of divine love, He teaches surrender, which is the life of the devotees.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dainya, ātma-nivedana, goptṛtve varaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>‘avaśya rakṣibe kṛṣṇa’—viśvāsa pālana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Humility, self-submission, embracing the Lord’s guardianship, maintaining the faith that ‘Kṛṣṇa will certainly protect me’ …</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakti-anukūla mātra kāryera svīkāra</strong><br>
<strong>bhakti-pratikūla bhāva—varjanāṅgīkāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Engaging only in activities that are favourable to devotion, rejecting everything unfavourable to devotion …</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ṣaḍ-aṅga śaraṇāgati ha-ibe yā̐hāra</strong><br>
<strong>tā̐hāra prārthanā śune śrī-nanda-kumāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Kṛṣṇa hears the prayers of one who surrenders in this six-fold way.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rūpa-sanātana-pade dante tṛṇa kari’</strong><br>
<strong>bhakativinoda paḍe dui pada dhari’</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bhakti Vinod places a straw between his teeth and falls before Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī Prabhu and Śrīla Sanātan Goswāmī Prabhu, clasping their feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kā̐diyā kā̐diyā bale, āmi ta’ adhama</strong><br>
<strong>śikhāye śaraṇāgati karahe uttama</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Crying and crying, he prays, “I am so lowly! Please elevate me by teaching me surrender.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-bhaja-re-bhaja-re-amar-mana-ati-manda" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Bhajan-gīti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhaja re bhaja re āmār mana ati manda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(bhajan vinā gati nāi re)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ohe āmār mūḍha-mana bhajan vinā gati nāi re)</strong><br>
<strong>(bhaja) vraja-vane rādhā-kṛṣṇa-charaṇāravinda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(jñāna-karma parihari’ re)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(śuddha rāga-patha dhari’ jñāna-karma parihari’ re)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Serve! Serve! My mind is so foolish. There is no way other than service! O my foolish mind! There is no way other than service. Serve Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet in Vṛndāvan. Abandon worldly knowledge and action! Follow the path of divine love and abandon worldly knowledge and action!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>(bhaja) gaura-gadādharādvaita-guru-nityānanda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(gaura-kṛṣṇe abhed jene)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(guru kṛṣṇa-priya jene gaura-kṛṣṇe abhed jene re)</strong><br>
<strong>śrīnivāsa, haridāsa, murāri, mukunda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(gaura-preme smara smara re)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(śrīnivāsa, haridāse, gaura-preme smara smara re)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Serve Gaurāṅga, Gadādhar, Advaita, Nityānanda, and Śrī Guru. Know that Gaurāṅga and Kṛṣṇa are nondifferent, and that Śrī Guru is Kṛṣṇa’s dear devotee. Remember Śrīvās Ṭhākur, Hari Dās Ṭhākur, Murāri Gupta, and Mukunda Datta. With love for Gaurāṅga, always remember them. With love for Gaurāṅga, remember Śrīvās Ṭhākur and Hari Dās Ṭhākur.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rūpa-sanātana-jīva-raghunātha-dvandva</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(yadi bhajan karbe re)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(rūpa-sanātane smara yadi bhajan karbe re)</strong><br>
<strong>rāghava-gopāla-bhaṭṭa-svarūpa-rāmānanda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(kṛṣṇa prema yadi chāo re)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(svarūpa-rāmānande smara kṛṣṇa-prema yadi chāo re)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Remember Rūpa Goswāmī, Sanātan Goswāmī, Jīva Goswāmī, Raghunāth Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, and Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī. If you want to serve, then remember Śrī Rūpa and Śrī Sanātan. Remember Rāghava Paṇḍit, Gopāl Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, Svarūp Dāmodar, and Rāmānanda Rāy. If you want Kṛṣṇa-prema, then remember Svarūp Dāmodar and Rāmānanda Rāy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>goṣṭhī-saha karṇapura sena-śivānanda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ajasra smara smara re)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(goṣṭhī-saha sena śivānande ajasra smara re)</strong><br>
<strong>rūpānuga sādhu-jana bhajana-ānanda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(vraje vās yadi chāo re)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(rūpānuga sādhu-jane smara vraje vās yadi chāo re)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Remember Kavi Karṇapur, Śivānanda Sen, and their family. Always remember them! Always remember Śivānanda Sen and his family. Remember the sādhus who follow Śrī Rūpa and the joy they feel in their service. If you want to reside in Vraja, then remember the sādhus who follow Śrī Rūpa.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-vibhavari-shiesha-aloka-praveshia" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Vibhāvarī Śeṣa <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vibhāvarī śeṣa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āloka praveśa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nidrā chhāḍi’ uṭha jīva</strong><br>
<strong>bala hari hari&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mukunda murāri</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rāma kṛṣṇa hayagrīva</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O souls! The night has ended and the light has come. Awake and arise! Chant ‘Hari! Hari!’ The Supreme Lord Kṛṣṇa is the bestower of liberation, the demon Mura’s enemy, and Śrī Rādhā’s lover. He appears as Hayagrīva, the horse-headed Avatār.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nṛsiṁha vāmana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-madhusūdana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vrajendra-nandana śyāma</strong><br>
<strong>pūtanā-ghātana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kaiṭabha-śātana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jaya dāśarathi-rāma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to Kṛṣṇa! He appears as Nṛsiṁhadev, the half-man, half-lion Avatār, the dwarf Avatār, Vāman, and the son of Daśarath, Rāmachandra. He is the destroyer of the demons Madhu, Pūtanā, and Kaiṭabha. He is the King of Vraja’s son and blackish in complexion.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yaśodā dulāla&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;govinda gopāla</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vṛndāvana purandara</strong><br>
<strong>gopī-priya-jana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rādhikā-ramaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhuvana-sundara-vara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is Mother Yaśodā’s darling son, and the protector and delight of the cows. He is the Lord of Vṛndāvan, the gopīs’ beloved, Rādhikā’s lover, and the most beautiful person in all the worlds.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rāvāṇāntakara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mākhana-taskara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopī-jana-vastra-hārī</strong><br>
<strong>vrajera rākhāla&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopa-vṛnda-pāla</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chita-hārī vaṁśī-dhārī</strong></p>
