<p class="translation">As Rāmachandra, He is the destroyer of Rāvaṇ. Krṣṇa is a butter-thief and the stealer of the gopīs’ clothes. He is a cowherd boy in Vraja, and the protector of the other cowherd boys. He is a flute player who steals the hearts of all.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yogīndra-vandana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-nanda-nandana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vraja-jana-bhaya-hārī</strong><br>
<strong>navīna nīrada&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rūpa manohara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mohana-vaṁśī-vihārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Nanda Mahārāj’s son, Kṛṣṇa, is worshiped by the greatest yogīs. He is the remover of all fear from the people of Vraja. He is an enchanting flute player whose heart-capturing form is the colour of a fresh rain cloud.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yaśodā-nandana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kaṁsa-nisūdana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nikuñja-rāsa-vilāsī</strong><br>
<strong>kadamba-kānana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rāsa-parāyaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vṛndā-vipina-nivāsī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Yaśodā’s son Kṛṣṇa is the killer of Kaṁsa and the enjoyer of the Rāsa Dance in the forest. He resides in Vṛndāvan and performs the rāsa dance beneath the kadamba trees.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ānanda-vardhana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prema-niketana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;phula-śara-yojaka kāma</strong><br>
<strong>gopāṅgaṇā-gaṇa-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chita-vinodana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;samasta-guṇa-gaṇa-dhāma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is ever-increasing joy personified and the ultimate reservoir of divine love. He is a divine Cupid, who captures His beloveds with flower arrows. He is the pleasure of the gopīs’ hearts and the abode of all wonderful qualities.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yāmuna-jīvana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;keli-parāyaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mānasa-chandra-chakora</strong><br>
<strong>nāma-sudhā-rasa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gāo kṛṣṇa-yaśa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rākha vachana mana mora</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He plays in the River Yamunā’s waters and is the moon above the chakora bird of the heart. O mind, remember my words: always chant Kṛṣṇa’s glories and nectarean Names.</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section class="category">
<h1>Evening</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-shri-guru-charana-padma-kevala-bhakati" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Guru-vaiṣṇava Māhātmya-gīti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī guru charaṇa-padma&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kevala bhakati-sadma</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vando̐ muñi sāvadhāna mate</strong><br>
<strong>yā̐hāra prasāde bhāi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;e bhava tariyā yāi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa-prāpti haya yā̐hā haite</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I carefully offer my obeisance to Śrī Guru’s lotus feet, which are the sole abode of devotion. O brother! By his mercy we cross over this world and reach Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>guru-mukha-padma-vākya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chitete kariyā aikya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āra nā kariha mane āśā</strong><br>
<strong>śrī guru-charaṇe rati&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ei se uttama-gati</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ye prasāde pūre sarva āśā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Make the teachings from Śrī Guru’s lotus mouth one with your heart, and do not desire anything else within your heart. Attachment to Śrī Guru’s feet is the best path. By his mercy all desires are fulfilled.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chakṣu-dāna dilā yei&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;janme janme prabhu sei</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;divya-jñāna hṛde prakāśita</strong><br>
<strong>prema-bhakti yā̐hā haite&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;avidyā vināśa yāte</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vede gāya yā̐hāra charita</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He who gave me the gift of eyes is my lord, birth after birth. He manifested divine knowledge in my heart. He gives prema-bhakti and destroys ignorance. The Vedas sing of his character.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-guru karuṇā-sindhu&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;adhama janāra bandhu</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;lokanātha lokera jīvana</strong><br>
<strong>hā hā prabhu kara dayā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;deha more pada-chhāyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;e adhama la-ila śaraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Guru is an ocean of mercy and the friend of the fallen. He is the lord and life of all people. O master! Be merciful. Give me the shade of your feet. This fallen soul has taken shelter of you.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>(āmi) (śaraṇ nilām)</strong><br>
<strong>(abhaya-śrī-pāda-padme&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śaraṇ nilām)</strong><br>
<strong>(āmāy) (dayā kara he)</strong><br>
<strong>(patit-pāvan śrī gurudev&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dayā kara he)</strong><br>
<strong>(adhama patita jene&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dayā kara he)</strong><br>
<strong>(jay gurudev)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have taken shelter. I have taken shelter at your lotus feet, where there is no fear. Please be merciful to me. O Śrī Gurudev, saviour of the fallen, please be merciful to me. Understanding that I am lowly and fallen, be merciful to me. All glory to you, Śrī Gurudev!</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shri-krishna-chaitanya-prabhu-daya-kara-more" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Sāvaraṇa-Śrī-Gaura-pāda-padme Prārthanā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī kṛṣṇa chaitanya prabhu dayā kara more</strong><br>
<strong>tomā vinā ke dayālu jagata mājhāre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Śrī Kṛṣṇa Chaitanya Mahāprabhu! Be merciful to me. Other than You, who is merciful in this world?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>patita-pāvana-hetu tava avatāra</strong><br>
<strong>mo sama patita prabhu nā pāibe āra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You descended to save the fallen souls. O Lord, You will not find another soul as fallen as I.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hā hā prabhu nityānanda premānanda sukhī</strong><br>
<strong>kṛpāvalokana kara āmi baḍa duḥkhī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Nityānanda! You are overjoyed with divine love. Give me Your merciful glance. I am very sad.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dayā kara sītā-pati advaita gosāñi</strong><br>
<strong>tava kṛpā-bale pāi chaitanya-nitāi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Advaita Gosāi, Lord of Sītā Ṭhākurāṇī! Be merciful to me. By Your mercy, I will reach Śrī Chaitanya and Nitāi.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaura-premamaya-tanu paṇḍit gadādhara</strong><br>
<strong>śrīnivāsa haridāsa dayāra sāgara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gadādhar Paṇḍit, embodiment of love for Śrī Gaura! O oceans of mercy, Śrīvās Paṇḍit and Hari Dās Ṭhākur!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hā hā svarūpa sanātana rūpa raghunātha</strong><br>
<strong>bhaṭṭa-yuga śrī jīva hā prabhu lokanātha</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Svarūp Dāmodar, Sanātan, Rūpa, Raghunāth Dās, Raghunāth Bhaṭṭa, Gopāl Bhaṭṭa, and Śrī Jīva! O Lokanāth Prabhu!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dayā kara śrī-āchārya prabhu śrīnivāsa</strong><br>
<strong>rāmachandra-saṅga māge narottama dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Śrīnivās Āchārya Prabhu! Be merciful! Narottam Dās prays for the association of Rāmachandra Kavirāj.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>(dayā kara prabhupāda śrī-gaura-prakāśa)</strong><br>
<strong>(tava jana kṛpā māge ei adhama dāsa)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Śrīla Prabhupād, manifestation of Śrī Gaura! Be merciful. This fallen servant prays for the mercy of your associates.</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section class="category">
<h1>Vaiṣṇava</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-vaishnava-thakura-dayara-sagara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Vaiṣṇava-mahimā-gīti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>(ohe) vaiṣṇava ṭhākura&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dayāra sāgara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;e dāse karuṇā kari</strong><br>
<strong>diyā pada-chāyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śodha he āmāya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra charaṇa dhari</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O worshippable Vaiṣṇava, ocean of grace, be merciful to this servant. Give me the shade of your feet and purify me. I clasp your feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chhaya vega dami’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chhaya doṣa śodhi’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chhaya guṇa deha’ dāse</strong><br>
<strong>chhaya sat-saṅga&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;deha’ he āmāre</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;basechhi saṅgera āśe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Subjugate the six urges, rectify the six defects, and bestow the six good qualities upon this servant. Grant me the six kinds of good association. I have sat at your feet, desiring that association.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ekākī āmāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāhi pāya bala</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hari-nāma-saṅkīrtane</strong><br>
<strong>tumi kṛpā kari&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śraddhā-bindu diyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;deha’ kṛṣṇa-nāma-dhane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Alone I do not have the ability to properly chant the Lord’s Name. Mercifully give me a drop of faith and bestow upon me the wealth of Kṛṣṇa’s Name.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛṣṇa se tomāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa dite pāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra śakati āchhe</strong><br>
<strong>āmi ta’ kāṅgāla&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa’ bali</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dhāi tava pāchhe pāchhe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Kṛṣṇa is yours. You can give Kṛṣṇa. You have that power. I am very poor. Calling, ‘Kṛṣṇa!’ ‘Kṛṣṇa!’, I run after you.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>(āmi) (dhāi tava pāchhe pāchhe)</strong><br>
<strong>(kṛṣṇa dhana pābar āśe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dhāi tava pāchhe pāchhe)</strong><br>
<strong>(tumi dile kṛṣṇa dite pāra)</strong><br>
<strong>(kṛṣṇa tomār hṛdayer dhan&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tumi dileo dite pāra)</strong><br>
<strong>(kṛṣṇa debār śakti-dhara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tumi dileo dite pāra)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I run after you. I run after you in hope of attaining the wealth of Kṛṣṇa. If you desire to give Kṛṣṇa, you can give Him. If you desire to give, you can give. Kṛṣṇa is the wealth of your heart. You have the power to give Kṛṣṇa. If only you desire to give Kṛṣṇa, you can give Him.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-thakura-vaishnava-gana-kori-ei-nivedana" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Ṭhākura-vaiṣṇava-gaṇa <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ṭhākura vaiṣṇava-gaṇa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kari ei nivedana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mo baḍa adhama durāchāra</strong><br>
<strong>dāruṇa-saṁsāra-nidhi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhe ḍubāila vidhi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;keśe dhari’ more kara pāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O worshippable Vaiṣṇavas, I offer you this prayer. I am very fallen and misbehaved. Destiny has immersed me in the fearsome ocean of material existence. Please grab me by the hair and pull me to the shore.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vidhi baḍa balavān&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā śune dharama-jñāna</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadāi karama-pāśe bāndhe</strong><br>
<strong>nā dekhi tāraṇa leśa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yata dekhi saba kleśa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;anātha kātare te̐i kānde</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Destiny is very powerful. It does not listen to my knowledge of religion and always binds me with the ropes of karma. I do not see even a trace of hope. All I see is hardship. Thus helpless and distressed, I cry.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kāma, krodha, lobha, moha, mada, abhimāna saha</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āpana āpana sthāne ṭāne</strong><br>
<strong>aichāna āmāra mana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;phire yena andha jana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;supatha vipatha nāhi jāne</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lust, anger, greed, illusion, pride, and ego all pull me in their own directions. Thus my mind wanders like a blind man who does not know the right way from the wrong way.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nā la-inu sat mata&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;asate majila chita</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tuyā pāye nā karinu āśa</strong><br>
<strong>narottama dāse kaya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dekhi’ śuni’ lage bhaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tarāiyā laha nija pāśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have never accepted the proper conception. My mind has been immersed in illusion, and I have never aspired for your feet. Narottām Dās says, “Seeing and hearing about my situation, I am stricken with fear. Please rescue me and keep me by your side.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-ki-rupe-paibo-seva-mui-durachara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Ki-rūpe pāiba sevā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ki-rūpe pāiba sevā muñi durāchāra</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-guru-vaiṣṇave rati nā haila āmāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">How will I attain service? I am a sinner and have no attachment to Śrī Guru or the Vaiṣṇavas.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>aśeṣa māyāte mana magana ha-ila</strong><br>
<strong>vaiṣṇavete leśa-mātra rati nā janmila</strong></p>
<p class="translation">My mind has become immersed in neverending illusion. I do not have even a trace of attachment to the Vaiṣṇavas.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>viṣaye bhuliyā andha ha-inu divā-niśi</strong><br>
<strong>gale phā̐sa dite phere māyā se piśāchī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Immersed in the mundane day and night, I have forgotten you and become blind. The witch Māyā has come to place a noose around my neck.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>māyāre kariyā jaya chhāḍāna nā yāya</strong><br>
<strong>sādhu-kṛpā vinā āra nāhika upāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">It is impossible to overcome and leave behind Māyā without the sādhus’ mercy. There is no other way.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>adoṣa-daraśi-prabhu patita-uddhāra</strong><br>
<strong>ei-bāra narottame karaha nistāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O master who overlooks my faults! O saviour of the fallen! This time deliver Narottam Dās.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-thakura-vaishnava-pada-avanira" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Ṭhākura-vaiṣṇava-pada <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ṭhākura-vaiṣṇava-pada&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;avanīra susampada</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śuna bhāi! hañā eka mana</strong><br>
<strong>āśraya la-iyā bhaje&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāre kṛṣṇa nāhi tyaje</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āra saba mare akāraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The worshippable Vaiṣṇavas’ feet are the greatest treasure in the world. O brother, listen attentively: take shelter of the Vaiṣṇavas and serve. Kṛṣṇa does not reject one who does so. All others die in vain.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vaiṣṇava-charaṇa-jala&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prema-bhakti dite bala</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āra keha nahe balavanta</strong><br>
<strong>vaiṣṇava-charaṇa-renu&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mastake bhūṣaṇa vinu</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āra nāhi bhūṣaṇera anta</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The Vaiṣṇavas’ foot-water has the power to give one premabhakti. Nothing else has such power. I wear no ornament on my head other than the Vaiṣṇavas’ footdust.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tīrtha-jala-pavitra-guṇe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;likhiyāchhe purāṇe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se saba bhaktira pravañchana</strong><br>
<strong>vaiṣṇavera pādadoka&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sama nahe ei saba</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yāte haya vāñchhita pūraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The purifying power of water at places of pilgrimage has been described in the Purāṇas. Such statements are a devotional trick. Water from pilgrimage sites is never equal to the Vaiṣṇavas’ foot-water, which fulfils all of one’s desires.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vaiṣṇava-saṅgete mana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ānandita anukṣaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā haya kṛṣṇa-parasaṅga</strong><br>
<strong>dīna narottama kā̐nde&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hiyā dhairya nāhi bāndhe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mora daśā kena ha-ila bhaṅga</strong></p>
<p class="translation">My mind is always joyful in the company of the Vaiṣṇavas, where discussion of Kṛṣṇa is always happening. Humble Narottam cries, “My heart has no patience. Why has my condition changed? (Why have I lost the Vaiṣṇavas’ association?)”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-ei-vara-karuna-kara-vaishnava-gosani" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Vaiṣṇava Vijñāpti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ei-bāra karuṇā kara vaiṣṇava gosāi</strong><br>
<strong>patita-pāvana tomā vine keha nāi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O worshippable Vaiṣṇava, be merciful to me this time. O saviour of the fallen, without you there is no one.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kāhāra nikaṭe gele pāpa dūre yāya</strong><br>
<strong>emana dayāla prabhu kebā kothā pāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">If one comes to you, one’s sins go away. Where can such a merciful master be found?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaṅgāra-paraśa ha-ile paśchāte pāvana</strong><br>
<strong>darśane pavitra kara—ei tomāra guṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">If the Ganges touches someone, he is later purified, but you purify souls simply by seeing them—such is your power.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari-sthāne aparādhe tāre hari-nāma</strong><br>
<strong>tomā-sthāne aparādhe nāhika eḍāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">If one offends the Lord, the Lord’s Name delivers him, but if one offends you, one has no salvation.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra hṛdaye sadā govinda-viśrāma</strong><br>
<strong>govinda kahena—mama vaiṣṇava parāṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Govinda always resides in your heart, and He says, “My devotees are My heart.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>prati janme kari āśā charaṇera dhūli</strong><br>
<strong>narottame kara dayā āpnāra bali’</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In every birth I aspire for the dust of your feet. Be merciful to Narottam, considering him your own.</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section class="category">
<h1>Nitāi</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-nitai-pada-kamala-koti-chandra-sushitala" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Manaḥ-Śikṣā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nitāi-pada-kamala&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;koṭī-chandra-suśītala</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ye chhāyāya jagata juḍāya</strong><br>
<strong>hena nitāi vine bhāi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rādhā-kṛṣṇa pāite nāi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dṛḍha kari’ dhara nitāira pāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Cooling like ten million moons, Nitāi’s lotus feet soothe the world with their shade. O brother! Without Nitāi, you cannot reach Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa. Firmly grasp Nitāi’s feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>se sambandha nāhi yāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vṛthā janma gela tāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sei paśu baḍa durāchāra</strong><br>
<strong>nitāi nā balila mukhe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;majila saṁsāra-sukhe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vidyā-kule ki karibe tāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The life of one who had no connection with Nitāi passed in vain. Such an animal is very wicked. What will learning (aristocratic birth) do for one who was immersed in worldly pleasures and never called aloud, ‘Nitāi’?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ahaṅkāre matta haiñā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nitāi-pada pāsariyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;asatyere satya kari’ māni</strong><br>
<strong>nitāiyer karuṇā habe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vraje rādhā-kṛṣṇa pābe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dhara nitāiyer charaṇ du’khāni</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Maddened with pride, forgetting Nitāi’s feet, I accept untruth as truth. When Nitāi bestows His mercy you will reach Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa in Vraja. Grasp Nitāi’s feet!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nitāiyer charaṇa satya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā̐hāra sevaka nitya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nitāi-pada sadā kara āśa</strong><br>
<strong>narottama baḍa duḥkhī&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nitāi more kara sukhī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rākha rāṅgā-charaṇera pāśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Nitāi’s feet are truth, and His servants are eternal. Always aspire for Nitāi’s feet. Narottam is very sad: “O Nitāi! Make me happy. Keep me beside your reddish feet.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-nitai-gunamani-amara-nitai-gunamani" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Nitāi guṇamaṇi āmāra <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Lochan Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nitāi guṇamaṇi āmāra nitāi guṇamaṇi</strong><br>
<strong>āniyā premera vanyā bhāsāla avanī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Nitāi, the jewel of all good qualities, my Nitāi, the jewel of all good qualities, brought a flood of divine love and inundated the earth.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>prema-vanyā laye nitāi āila gauḍa-deśe</strong><br>
<strong>ḍubila bhakata-gaṇa dīna-hīna bhāse</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Nitāi brought a flood of divine love to the land of Gauḍa. The devotees immersed themselves in it, but the unfortunate floated on its surface.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dīna-hīna patita pāmara nāhi bāchhe</strong><br>
<strong>brahmāra durlabha prema sabākāre yāche</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The sinful, unfortunate, and fallen did not desire this divine love, which is difficult for even Brahmā to attain, yet Nitāi begged them to accept it.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ābaddha karuṇā-sindhu kāṭiyā muhāna</strong><br>
<strong>ghare ghare bule prema-amiyāra vāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Nitāi cut a canal into the sealed ocean of mercy and a flood of nectarean divine love spread to every home.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>lochan bale hena nitāi yebā nā bhajila</strong><br>
<strong>jāniyā śuniyā sei ātma-ghātī haila</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lochan Dās says, “Anyone who did not serve Nitāi knowingly committed suicide.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-akrodha-paramananda" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Akrodha paramānanda <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Lochan Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>akrodha paramānanda nityānanda rāya</strong><br>
<strong>abhimāna-śūnya nitāi nagare beḍāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The anger-less, prideless, supremely joyful Nityānanda Rāy wanders throughout the towns.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>adhama patita jīver dvāre dvāre giyā</strong><br>
<strong>hari-nāma mahāmantra dena bilāiyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Going from door to door to the homes of the lowly and fallen souls, He distributes the Hare Kṛṣṇa mahāmantra.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yāre dekhe tāre kahe dante tṛṇa dhari’</strong><br>
<strong>āmāre kiniyā laha bhaja gaurahari</strong></p>
<p class="translation">With straw between His teeth, He says to whoever He sees, “Purchase Me: simply serve Gaurahari.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>eta bali’ nityānanda bhūme gaḍi yāya</strong><br>
<strong>sonāra parvata yena dhūlāte loṭāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Saying this, Nityānanda rolls on the ground like a golden mountain rolling in the dust.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hena avatāre yāra rati nā janmila</strong><br>
<strong>lochan bale sei pāpī ela āra gela</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lochan Dās says of one who has not developed attachment to this Avatār, “Such a sinner simply came and went.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>(yāoyā āsā sār ha-ila)</strong><br>
<strong>(nitāi-charaṇ nā bhajila&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yāoyā āsā sār ha-ila)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“They simply came and went. Those who did not serve Nitāi’s feet simply came and went (took birth and died uselessly).”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-parama-karuna-pahu-dui-jana" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nityānander Dayā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Lochan Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>parama karuṇa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pah. dui jana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nitāi gaurachandra</strong><br>
<strong>saba avatāra-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sāra śiromaṇi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kevala ānanda-kanda</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Nitāi and Gaurachandra are the two most merciful Lords. They are the best, the crest-jewels, of all Avatārs and the only source of joy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>(kevala-i ānanda-kanda)</strong><br>
<strong>(nityānanda gaurachandra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kevala-i ānanda-kanda)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">They are the only source of joy. Nityānanda and Gaurachandra are the only source of joy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhaja bhaja bhāi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chaitanya nitāi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sudṛḍha viśvāsa kari’</strong><br>
<strong>viṣaya chhāḍiyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se rase majiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mukhe bala hari hari</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O brothers! Serve! Serve Śrī Śrī Chaitanya-Nitāi with firm faith! Leave aside worldly affairs, merge into this rasa, and chant aloud, ‘Hari! Hari!’</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>(mukhe bala gaurahari)</strong><br>
<strong>(viṣay-kathā parihari’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mukhe bala gaurahari)</strong><br>
<strong>(grāmya-kathā parihari’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mukhe bala gaurahari)</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="translation">Chant aloud, ‘Gaurahari’! Give up mundane talk and chant aloud ‘Gaurahari’! Give up village talk and chant aloud ‘Gaurahari’!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>dekha ore bhāi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tribhuvane nāi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;emana dayāla dātā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O brother! Look! There are no benefactors in the three worlds as merciful as Them.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>(eman dayāl ke vā āchhe)</strong><br>
<strong>(nitāira gaurer mata&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;eman dayāl ke vā āchhe)</strong><br>
<strong>(mar kheyeo nām prema yāche&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;eman dayāl ke vā āchhe)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Who else is so merciful? Who is as merciful as Nitāi and Gaura? They endure even being beaten, and yet They ask Their aggressors to accept the Holy Name and divine love. Who else is so merciful?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>paśu pākhī jhure&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pāṣāṇa vidare</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śuni yā̐ra guṇa-gāthā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Hearing of Their glory, birds and animals cry and stones melt.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>saṁsāre majiyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rahili paḍiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se pade nahila āśa</strong><br>
<strong>āpana karama&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhuñyāya śamana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kahaye lochana dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“Fallen and immersed in saṁsāra, I never aspired for Their feet. Now Yamarāj subjects me to my karma.” So says Lochan Dās.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-dayal-nitai-chaitanya-bole-nach-re" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Dayāl Nitāi Chaitanya <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>‘dayāl nitāi chaitanya’ ba’le nāch re āmār mana</strong><br>
<strong>nāch re āmār mana, nāch re āmār mana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O my mind, dance! O my mind, dance! O my mind, dance, chanting, “Dayāl Nitāi Chaitanya!”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>(eman dayāl ta’ nāi he mār kheye prema dey)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh! No one is so merciful! Nitāi endures being beaten yet gives His aggressors divine love.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>(ore) aparādha dūre yābe, pābe prema-dhan</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(aparādhera vichāra to nāi he)</strong><br>
<strong>(takhan) kṛṣṇa-nāme ruchi habe, ghuchibe bandhana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(anurāg to habe he)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Then my offences will go away and I will attain the wealth of divine love. (In the Names of Nitāi and Chaitanya, there is no consideration of offences.) Then I will develop taste for Kṛṣṇa’s Name and my bondage will be destroyed. (Then I will have loving attachment.)</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>(takhan) anāyāse saphal habe jīvera jīvana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(naile jīvan to michhe he)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(kṛṣṇa-rati vinā jīver jīvan to michhe he)</strong><br>
<strong>(śeṣe) vṛndāvane rādhā-śyāmer pābe daraśana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(gaura-kṛpā hale he)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Then easily the life of the soul will be successful. (Without attachment to Kṛṣṇa, a soul’s life is simply false.) Then, at the end of life, a soul will see Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Śyām in Vṛndāvan (if Śrī Gaura bestows His mercy).</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section class="category">
<h1>Gaurāṅga</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-gaurangera-duti-pada-jar-dhana-sampada" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Sāvaraṇa-Śrī-Gaura-mahimā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaurāṅgera duṭi pada&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yāra dhana sampada</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se jāne bhakati-rasa-sāra</strong><br>
<strong>gaurāṅgera madhura-līlā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yāra karṇe praveśilā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hṛdaya nirmala bhela tāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">One whose wealth is Śrī Gaurāṅga’s feet knows the essence of devotion’s rasa. The heart of one who hears Śrī Gaurāṅga’s sweet Pastimes becomes pure.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ye gaurāṅgera nāma laya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāra haya premodaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāre muñi yāi balihāri</strong><br>
<strong>gaurāṅga-guṇete jhure&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nitya-līlā tāre sphure</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se jana bhakati-adhikārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">One who chants Śrī Gaurāṅga’s Name develops divine love. To him, I say, “Bravo!” The eternal Pastimes are revealed to one who cries over Śrī Gaurāṅga’s qualities. Such a soul is qualified for devotion.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaurāṅgera saṅgi-gaṇe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nitya-siddha kari māne</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se yāya vrajendra-suta-pāśa</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-gauḍa-maṇḍala-bhūmi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yebā jāne chintāmaṇi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāra haya vraja-bhūme vāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">One who considers Śrī Gaurāṅga’s associates to be eternally liberated reaches Kṛṣṇa. One who knows the holy land of Gauḍa to be made of wish-fulfilling (spiritual) gemstone resides in the land of Vraja.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaura-prema-rasārṇave&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se taraṅge yebā ḍube</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se rādhā-mādhava-antaraṅga</strong><br>
<strong>gṛhe vā vanete thāke&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘hā gaurāṅga’ bale ḍāke</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;narottama māge tāra saṅga</strong></p>
<p class="translation">One who swims in the waves of the nectarean ocean of divine love for Śrī Gaura is an intimate associate of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava. Whether he lives in a house or in the forest, Narottam Dās begs for the association of one who calls, ‘O Gaurāṅga!’</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-emona-durmati-samsara-bhitore" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Dainyātmikā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>(prabhu he!)</strong><br>
<strong>emana durmati&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṁsāra bhitare</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;paḍiyā āchhinu āmi</strong><br>
<strong>tava nija-jana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kona mahājane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pāṭhāiyā dile tumi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord! I am very wicked-minded and have fallen into this material world, but You have sent a great soul, Your associate (to rescue me).</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dayā kari more&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;patita dekhiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kahila āmāre giyā</strong><br>
<strong>ohe dīna-jana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śuna bhāla kathā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ullasita ha’be hiyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Seeing me so fallen and being merciful, he came to me and said, “O humble soul, listen to this wonderful message and your heart will rejoice.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāre tārite&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-kṛṣṇa-chaitanya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;navadvīpe avatāra</strong><br>
<strong>tomā hena kata&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dīna-hīna jane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;karilena bhava-pāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“Śrī Kṛṣṇa Chaitanya has appeared in Nabadwīp to deliver you. He has brought many humble souls just like you to the shore of the ocean of material existence.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vedera pratijñā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rākhibāra tare</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rukma-varṇa vipra-suta</strong><br>
<strong>mahāprabhu nāme&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nadīyā mātāya</strong><br>
<strong>(mahāprabhu nāme&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jagata mātāya)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṅge bhāi avadhūta</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“To fulfil the prophecies of the Vedas, He has appeared with a golden complexion as a brāhmaṇ’s son bearing the name ‘Mahāprabhu’ and driven all of Nadia (all the world) mad with divine love in the company of His brother Nityānanda.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nanda suta yini&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chaitanya gosāñī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nija-nāma kari’ dāna</strong><br>
<strong>tārila jagat&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tumi-o yāiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;laha nija paritrāṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“Śrī Chaitanya, who is Kṛṣṇa Himself, has distributed His own Name and delivered the world. Go also and accept your deliverance.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>se kathā śuniyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āsiyāchhi, nātha!</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra charaṇa-tale</strong><br>
<strong>bhakati-vinoda&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kā̐diyā kā̐diyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āpana-kāhinī bale</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord, hearing this message, Bhakti Vinod has come to the soles of Your feet. Crying, he tells his story.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-gay-gora-madhur-svare" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Nāma <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gāya gorā madhur svare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Gaurasundar sings in a sweet voice:</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>“hare kṛṣṇa hare kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa hare hare</strong><br>
<strong>hare rāma hare rāma rāma rāma hare hare”</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gṛhe thāka, vane thāka</strong><br>
<strong>sadā ‘hari’ bale’ ḍāka</strong><br>
<strong>sukhe duḥkhe bhulo nāka</strong><br>
<strong>vadane hari-nām kara re</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Stay at home, or stay in a forest, regardless, always call out ‘Hari!’ In happiness and in sadness, don’t forget: chant the Lord’s Name aloud.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>māyā-jāle baddha ha’ye</strong><br>
<strong>āchha michhe kāja la’ye</strong><br>
<strong>ekhan-o chetana peye</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-mādhav-nām bala re</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bound in Māyā’s net, you work in vain. Now, while you are still conscious, chant ‘Rādhā-Mādhava’.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jīvana ha-ila śeṣa</strong><br>
<strong>nā bhajile hṛṣīkeśa</strong><br>
<strong>bhaktivinod-upadeśa</strong><br>
<strong>ek-bār nām-rase māta re</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Your life is ending, but you have not served Hṛṣīkeś. Bhakti Vinod advises: once relish His Name’s nectar.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-vimala-hemajin-tanu-anupama-re" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Vimala hema jini <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Vṛndāvan Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vimala hema jini&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tanu anupama re!</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhe śobhe nānā phula-dāma</strong><br>
<strong>kadamba keśara jini&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ekaṭī pulaka re!</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāra mājhe bindu bindu ghāma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">His unparalleled form surpasses the purest gold! He is decorated with a garland of assorted flowers. His erect hairs surpass kadamba filaments! He is covered with beads of sweat.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jini mada-matta hāti&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gamana manthara ati</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhāvāveśe ḍhuli ḍhuli yāya</strong><br>
<strong>aruṇa-vasana chhavi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jini prabhātera ravi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gorā-aṅge laharī khelāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">His slow movements surpass those of an intoxicated elephant. Rapt in ecstasy, He sways to and fro. The splendour of His saffron garments surpasses the morning sun. Waves play across His golden form.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chalite nā pāre gorā-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chā̐d go̐sāñi re</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;balite nā pāre ādha-bola</strong><br>
<strong>bhāvete āveśa haiyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hari hari bolāiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āchaṇḍāle dhari’ deya kola</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Gorāchā̐d cannot move! He cannot speak! He simply stammers. Rapt in ecstasy, He induces outcastes to chant ‘Hari! Hari!’ and He embraces them.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>e sukha-sampada-kāle&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gorā nā bhajinu hele</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hena pade nā karinu āśa</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-kṛṣṇa-chaitanya-chandraṭhākura śrī-nityānanda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;guṇa gāya vṛndāvana dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">At this joyful, fortunate time, neglectfully I did not serve Gorāchā̐d or aspire for His feet. Vṛndāvan Dās sings the glories of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Chaitanyachandra and Ṭhākur Śrī Nityānanda.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-emona-gauranga-bine-nahi-ara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Emana Gaurāṅga vine nāhi āra <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Premānanda Dās</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;emana gaurāṅga vine nāhi āra</strong><br>
<strong>hena avatāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;habe ki hayechhe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hena prema parachāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">There is no one like Gaurāṅga! Has there ever been and will there ever be an Avatār who distributes divine love like Him?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>(hay ni ār habār nay re)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh! There has never been and never will be!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>duramati ati&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;patita pāṣaṇḍī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prāṇe nā mārila kāre</strong><br>
<strong>hari-nāma diye&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hṛdaya śodhila</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yāchi giyā ghare ghare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He did not kill the fallen, wicked-hearted sinners; He gave them the Lord’s Name and purified their hearts. He went from house to house and begged them (to accept divine love).</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>(ke nibi ke nibi bale)</strong><br>
<strong>(yeche yeche yeche beḍāy)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“Who will take? Who will take? He goes out asking, asking, and asking.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhava-viriñchira&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vāñchhita ye prema</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jagate phelila ḍhāli</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(bhava viriñchira yā vāñchhā kare)</strong><br>
<strong>kāṅgale pāiye&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;khāila nāchiye</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bājāiye karatāli</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He freely distributed throughout the world divine love that is desired by even Brahmā and Śiva. The poor received and relished it, dancing and clapping their hands.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>(karatāli bājāiye)</strong><br>
<strong>(gaurahari hari bale&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;karatāli bājāiye)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Clapping their hands! Chanting, “Gaurahari Hari!” and clapping their hands!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hāsiye kā̐diye&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;preme gaḍāgaḍi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pulake vyāpila aṅga</strong><br>
<strong>chaṇḍāle brāhmaṇe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kare kolākuli</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kabe vā chhila e raṅga</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Filled with divine love, they laughed, cried, and rolled about. Their body hair stood on end. The brāhmaṇs and the outcastes embraced. When were there ever such Pastimes?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>(chaṇḍāle brāhmaṇe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kare kolākuli</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;e raṅga kabe vā chhila)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The brāhmaṇs and the outcastes embraced. When were there ever such Pastimes?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ḍākiye hā̐kiye&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;khola-karatāle</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gāiye dhāiye phire</strong><br>
<strong>dekhiyā śamana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tarāsa pāiye</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kapāṭa hānila dvāre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Howling and calling out, they sang and ran about with mṛdaṅgas and kartāls. Seeing this, Yamarāj became afraid and closed his doors and shutters.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>(bhaja bhaja gaurahari yā̐ra nāme śamaṇa palāy)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Serve! Serve Gaurahari! Yamarāj flees from His Name.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>e tina bhuvana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ānande bharila</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;uṭhila maṅgala-sora</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The three worlds filled with joy, and an auspicious tumult arose.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>(ānander ār sīmā nāi re)</strong><br>
<strong>(gauraharir āgamane&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ānander ār sīmā nāi re)</strong><br>
<strong>(nirānanda dūre gela&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ānander ār sīmā nāi re)</strong><br>
<strong>(gauraharir āgamane&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;premānander udaya hala)</strong><br>
<strong>(premānander udaya hala&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sabāi mile haribola)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">There is no limit to our joy! By Gaurahari’s arrival, there is no limit to our joy! All unhappiness has gone away, and there is no limit to our joy! By Gaurahari’s arrival, the joy of divine love has arisen, and everyone together chants “Hari!”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>kahe premānanda&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;emana gaurāṅge</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rati nā janmila mora</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Premānanda says, “I never developed attachment to Lord Gaurāṅga.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>(āmār rati-mati hala nā ye)</strong><br>
<strong>(eman dayāl prabhu avatāre, āmār rati-mati hala nā re)</strong><br>
<strong>(eman dayāl prabhu esechhila, āmār rati-mati hala nā re)</strong><br>
<strong>(gaurahari hari bala)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I never developed attachment. I never developed attachment to this most merciful Lord. This most merciful Lord has come, but I have never developed attachment to Him. Oh! Chant, “Gaurahari!”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-ke-jabi-ke-jabi-bhai-bhava-sindhu-para" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Ke yābi ke yābi <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Lochan Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ke yābi ke yābi bhāi bhava-sindhu pāra</strong><br>
<strong>dhanya kali-yuge re chaitanya-avatāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Who will cross, O brother, who will cross the ocean of material existence? Oh! The fortune of this Age of Kali is the appearance of Śrī Chaitanya.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āmāra gaurāṅger ghāṭe ujānakheyā vaya</strong><br>
<strong>kaḍipāti nāhi lāge amani pāra haya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">At my Lord Gaurāṅga’s ghāṭ the upstream ferry runs without requesting any fare. Everyone crosses freely.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari-nāmera tarīkhāni śrī-guru-kāṇḍārī</strong><br>
<strong>saṅkīrtana-keroyāla du’bāhu pasāri</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The Lord’s Names are the ferryboat, Śrī Guru is the captain, and the outstretched arms in the saṅkīrtan are the oars.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sarva-jīva uddhāra haila premera vātāse</strong><br>
<strong>lochana paḍiyā raila karamera doṣe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All souls have thus been delivered by the winds of divine love. Only Lochan Dās has fallen and remained behind, as a result of his sins.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-avatara-sara-gora-avatara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Avatāra Sāra <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Lochan Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>avatāra sāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gorā avatāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kena nā bhajili tā̐re</strong><br>
<strong>kari’ nīre vāsa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gela nā piyāsa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āpana karama phere</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Gaura Avatār is the best of all Avatārs. Why don’t you serve Him? O mind, you resided beside water, but did not quench your thirst. You returned to your selfishness.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kaṇṭakera taru&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadāi sevili (mana)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;amṛta pā’bāra āśe</strong><br>
<strong>prema-kalpataru&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī gaurāṅga āmāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhāre bhavili viṣe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O mind, desiring to drink nectar, you constantly chewed thorned wood (and mistook the blood in your mouth for nectar). My Lord Gaurāṅga is a wish-fulfilling tree of divine love, but you considered Him to be poison.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>saurabhera āśe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;palāśa ś.kili (mana)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāsāte paśila kīṭa</strong><br>
<strong>‘ikṣu-daṇḍa’ bhāvi’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kāṭha chuṣili (mana)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kemane pāibi miṭha</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O mind, seeking a fragrance, you smelled the odourless palāśa flower, and insects entered your nose. O mind, considering it sugarcane, you sucked dry wood. How will you taste sweetness in this way?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>‘hāra’ baliyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;galāya parili (mana)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śamana kiṅkara sāpa</strong><br>
<strong>‘śītala’ baliyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āguna pohāli (mana)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pāili vajara-tāpa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O mind, considering it a necklace, you wore death’s attendant serpent around your neck. O mind, considering it to be cooling, you embraced fire and received a scorching burn.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>saṁsāra bhajili&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-gaurāṅga bhulili</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā śunili sādhura kathā</strong><br>
<strong>iha-parakāla&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dukāla khoyāli (mana)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;khāili āpana māthā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You served your family (mundane ends), forgot Śrī Gaurāṅga, and never listened to the sādhus’ words. O mind, you wasted both this life and your next life. You depraved yourself.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-gaura-pahu-na-bhajiya-mainu" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Gaurā Pahu̐ <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaurā pahu̐ nā bhajiyā mainu</strong><br>
<strong>prema ratana-dhana helāya hārāinu</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Not serving Lord Gaurāṅga, I have died. Negligently I have lost the treasured jewel of divine love.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>adhane yatana kari dhana teyāginu</strong><br>
<strong>āpana karama-doṣe āpani ḍubinu</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have endeavoured for worthless things and rejected real wealth. I have drowned myself in my own sins.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sat-saṅga chhāḍi’ kainu asate vilāsa</strong><br>
<strong>te-kāraṇe lāgila ye karma-bandha-phā̐sa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have avoided the sādhus’ association and enjoyed with wicked people. Thus I have become bound in the noose of my karma.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>viṣaya-viṣama-viṣa satata khāinu</strong><br>
<strong>gaura-kīrtana rase magana nā hainu</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have constantly drunk the terrible poison of mundanity and never become immersed in the nectar of Śrī Gaurāṅga’s kīrtan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kena vā āchhaye prāṇa ki sukha lāgiyā</strong><br>
<strong>narottama dāsa kena nā gela mariyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Why am I alive? For the sake of what happiness? Why has Narottam Dās not already died?</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jadi-gaura-na-hoto-tobe-ki-hoito" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Yadi Gaura nā ho’to <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Vāsudev Ghoṣ</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yadi, gaura nā ha’ta&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tabe ki ha-ita</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kemane dharitām de</strong><br>
<strong>rādhāra mahimā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prema-rasa-sīmā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jagate jānāta ke?</strong></p>
<p class="translation">If Śrī Gaura had not come, what would have happened? How would we have maintained our lives? Who would have revealed to the world Śrī Rādhā’s glory and the extremes of Her ecstatic divine love?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>(rādhār mahimā ke jānata)</strong><br>
<strong>(gaura yadi nā āsita rādhār mahimā ke jānata)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Who would have known Śrī Rādhā’s glory? If Śrī Gaura would not have come, who would have known Śrī Rādhā’s glory?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>madhura vṛndā-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vipina mādhurī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;praveśa chāturī sāra</strong><br>
<strong>varaja-yuvatī&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhāvera bhakati</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śakati ha-ita kāra?</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Who would have the great expertise needed to enter into the sweetness of sweet Vṛndāvan? Who would have any qualification for the Vraja-gopīs’ ecstatic devotion?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gāo punaḥ punaḥ&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gaurāṅgera guṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sarala kariyā mana</strong><br>
<strong>e bhava-sāgare&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;emana dayāla</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā dekhiye eka-jana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Sing Gaurāṅga’s glories again and again, keeping your heart simple. In the ocean of this material world, no one has ever seen anyone so merciful.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>(āmi) gaurāṅga baliyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā genu galiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kemane dharinu de</strong><br>
<strong>vāsura-hiyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pāṣāṇa diyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(vidhi) kemane gaḍiyāchhe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I chant ‘Gaurāṅga’, but have not melted (with love). How have I maintained this body? How has Providence made Vāsu a stone rather than a heart?</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section class="category">
<h1>Kṛṣṇa</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-gopinath-mama-nivedana-shiuno" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Nivedana <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Part 1</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopīnāth, mama nivedana śuna</strong><br>
<strong>viṣayī durjana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā kāma-rata</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kichhu nāhi mora guṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gopīnāth, hear my prayer. I am a wicked, constantly covetous materialist. I do not have any good qualities.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopīnāth, āmāra bharasā tumi</strong><br>
<strong>tomāra charaṇe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;la-inu śaraṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra kiṅkara āmi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gopīnāth, You are my hope. I have taken shelter at Your feet. I am Your servant.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopīnāth, kemane śodhibe more</strong><br>
<strong>nā jāni bhakati&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;karme jaḍa-mati</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;paḍechhi saṁsāra ghore</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gopīnāth, how will You purify me? I do not understand devotion and my materialistic mind is absorbed in karma. I have fallen into this dark world.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopīnāth, sakali tomāra māyā</strong><br>
<strong>nāhi mama bala&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jñāna sunirmala</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;svādhīna nahe e kāyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gopīnāth, everything is Your illusion. I have no strength or transcendental knowledge, and this body of mine is not independent (of material nature’s control).</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopīnāth, niyata charaṇe sthāna</strong><br>
<strong>māge e pāmara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kā̐diyā kā̐diyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;karahe karuṇā dāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gopīnāth, weeping and weeping, this sinner begs for an eternal place at Your feet. Please give him Your mercy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopīnāth, tumi ta sakali pāra</strong><br>
<strong>durjane tārite&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra śakati</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ke āchhe pāpīra āra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gopīnāth, You can do anything. You have the power to deliver sinners, and who is a greater sinner than myself?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopīnāth, tumi kṛpā-pārābāra</strong><br>
<strong>jīvera kāraṇe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āsiyā prapañche</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;līlā kaile suvistāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gopīnāth, You are an ocean of mercy. You came into this world and performed Your Pastimes for the sake of the fallen souls.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopīnāth, āmi ki doṣera doṣī</strong><br>
<strong>asura sakala&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pāila charaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vinoda thākila vasi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gopīnāth, am I the greatest sinner of all? All the demons reached Your feet, and only this Bhakti Vinod remained sitting here.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-gopinath-ghuchao-samsara-jvala" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Nivedana <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Part 2</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopīnāth, ghuchāo saṁsāra-jvālā</strong><br>
<strong>avidyā-yātanā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āra nāhi sahe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;janama-maraṇa-mālā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gopīnāth, remove the suffering of saṁsāra. I can no longer tolerate the pain of ignorance or the cycle of birth and death.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopīnāth, āmi ta’ kāmera dāsa</strong><br>
<strong>viṣaya-vāsanā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jāgichhe hṛdaye</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;phā̐dichhe karama phā̐sa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gopīnāth, I am a servant of lust. Worldly desires have awakened in my heart, and the noose of karma has tightened around my neck.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopīnāth, kabe vā jāgiba āmi</strong><br>
<strong>kāma-rūpa ari&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dūre teyāgiba</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hṛdaye sphuribe tumi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gopīnāth, when will I wake up? When will I abandon my enemy—lust? When will You appear in my heart?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopīnāth, āmi ta’ tomāra jana</strong><br>
<strong>tomāre chhāḍiyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṁsāra bhajinu</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhuliyā āpana-dhana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gopīnāth, I am Yours, but I have abandoned You and engaged in worldly life, forgetting my actual wealth.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopīnāth, tumi ta’ sakali jāna</strong><br>
<strong>āpanāra jane&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;daṇḍiyā ekhana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-charaṇe deha sthāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gopīnāth, You know everything. Having now punished Your servant, give him a place at Your holy feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopīnāth, ei ki vichāra tava</strong><br>
<strong>vimukha dekhiyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chhāḍa nija-jane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā kara karuṇā-lava</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gopīnāth, is this Your judgement? Seeing me averse to You, You abandon Your servant and do not grant him even a trace of mercy?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopīnāth, āmi ta’ mūrakha ati</strong><br>
<strong>kise bhāla haya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kabhu nā bujhinu</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāi hena mama gati</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gopīnāth, I am very foolish. I have never understood what is good for me. Therefore my condition is such.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopīnāth, tumi ta’ paṇḍita-vara</strong><br>
<strong>mūḍhera maṅgala&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā anveṣibe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;e dāse nā bhāva para</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gopīnāth, You are the wisest person. Always try to uplift this fool and do not consider this servant an outsider.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-gopinath-amara-upaya-nai" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Nivedana <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Part 3</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopīnāth, āmāra upāya nāi</strong><br>
<strong>tumi kṛpā kari&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āmāre la-ile</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṁsāre uddhāra pāi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gopīnāth, I have no way out. Only if You mercifully take me will I be delivered from this world.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopīnāth, paḍechhi māyāra phere</strong><br>
<strong>dhana, dārā, suta&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ghirechhe āmāre</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kāmete rekhechhe jere</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gopīnāth, I have fallen into Māyā’s perils. Wealth, wife, and children have surrounded me, and lust has ruined me.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopīnāth, mana ye pāgala mora</strong><br>
<strong>nā māne śāsana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā achetana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viṣaye rayechhe bhora</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gopīnāth, my mind is crazy and does not submit to discipline. It is always senseless and has remained engrossed in mundanity.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopīnāth, hāra ye menechhi āmi</strong><br>
<strong>aneka yatana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ha-ila viphala</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ekhana bharasā tumi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gopīnāth, I have accepted defeat. All of my endeavours were useless. Now You are my hope.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopīnāth, kemane ha-ibe gati</strong><br>
<strong>prabala indriya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vaśī-bhūta mana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā chhāḍe viṣaya-rati</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gopīnāth, how shall I progress? My mind is controlled by my powerful senses and does not abandon its attachment to mundanity.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopīnāth, hṛdaye vasiyā mora</strong><br>
<strong>manake śamiyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;laha nija-pāne</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ghuchibe vipada ghora</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gopīnāth, sit down in my heart, subdue my mind, and bring me towards You. Dispel all danger and darkness.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopīnāth, anātha dekhiyā more</strong><br>
<strong>tumi hṛṣīkeśa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hṛṣīka damiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāra’ he saṁsṛti-ghore</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gopīnāth, You are the Lord of the senses. Seeing me so helpless subdue my senses and deliver me from this dark world.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopīnāth, galāya legechhe phā̐sa</strong><br>
<strong>kṛpā-asi dhari’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bandhana chhediyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vinode karaha dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gopīnāth, a noose has been tied around my neck. Taking up the sword of Your mercy, cut away this bondage and make Bhakti Vinod Your humble servant.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>(he govinda! gopīnātha!)</strong><br>
<strong>(ei bār āmāy dayā kara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;he govinda! gopīnātha!)</strong><br>
<strong>(bahu janme aparādhī&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;he govinda! gopīnātha!)</strong><br>
<strong>(lakṣa lakṣa janam gela&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;he govinda! gopīnātha!)</strong><br>
<strong>(śrī charaṇe śaraṇ nilām&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;he govinda! gopīnātha!)</strong><br>
<strong>(śrī charaṇe sevā diyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;he govinda! gopīnātha!)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Govinda! O Gopīnāth! This time be merciful. I have been an offender for many births. Million of births have passed. Give me the shade of Your feet. I have surrendered at Your feet. Give me the service of Your feet. O Govinda! O Gopīnāth!</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-hari-he-dayala-mora-jaya-radha-natha" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Dainya O Prapatti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari he dayāla mora jaya rādhā-nātha</strong><br>
<strong>bāra bāra ei-bāra laha nija sātha</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O merciful Lord! All glory to You, Rādhā’s Lord! Again and again I pray to You. This time take me as Your own.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bahu yoni bhrami’ nātha! la-inu śaraṇa</strong><br>
<strong>nija-guṇe kṛpā kara adhama tāraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord! After wandering through many wombs, I have taken shelter of You. Be merciful and deliver this fallen soul by Your divine power.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jagata-kāraṇa tumi jagata-jīvana</strong><br>
<strong>tomā chhāḍā kāro na’hi he rādhā-ramaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You are the world’s cause and life. Without You, no one has anything, O Lover of Rādhā!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhuvana-maṅgala tumi bhuvanera pati</strong><br>
<strong>tumi upekṣile nātha! ki ha-ibe gati</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You are the world’s auspiciousness and master. O Lord! If You neglect me, what will be my fate?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhāviyā dekhinu ei jagata mājhāre</strong><br>
<strong>tomā vinā keha nāhi e dāse uddhāre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have understood that in this world there is no one but You to deliver this servant.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-manasa-deho-geho-jo-kichu-mora" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Mānasa Deha Geha <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mānasa, deha, geha, yo kichhu mora</strong><br>
<strong>arpilu̐ tuyā pade nanda-kiśora!</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have offered my mind, my body, my household, and whatever else may be mine at Your feet, O Nanda Kiśor!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sampade vipade jīvane-maraṇe</strong><br>
<strong>dāy mama gelā tuyā o-pada varaṇe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In good times and in bad, in life and in death, all my responsibility has gone away by embracing Your feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mārabi rākhabi—yo ichhā tohārā</strong><br>
<strong>nitya-dāsa prati tuyā adhikārā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Kill me or protect me as You wish. You have full authority over Your eternal servant.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>janmāobi maye ichhā yadi tora</strong><br>
<strong>bhakta-gṛhe jani janma ha-u mora</strong></p>
<p class="translation">If You desire that I take birth again, let me do so in the home of Your devotee.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kīṭa-janma ha-u yathā tuyā dāsa</strong><br>
<strong>bahir-mukha brahma-janme nāhi āśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Let me be born again even as a worm, so long as I can remain Your servant. I have no desire to be born even as Brahmā if I will be averse to You.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhukti-mukti-spṛhā vihīna ye bhakta</strong><br>
<strong>labha-ite tā̐ka saṅga anurakta</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I long to attain the association of devotees who are completely free from all desire for selfish enjoyment and liberation.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>janaka, jananī, dayita, tanaya</strong><br>
<strong>prabhu, guru, pati—tuhu̐ sarva-maya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Father, mother, lover, son, Lord, Guru, and husband—You are everything to me.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakati-vinoda kahe, śuna kāna!</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-nātha! tuhu̐ hāmāra parāṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bhakti Vinod says, “Please listen, O Kān! O Rādhānāth! You are my life and soul.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-bhajahu-re-mana-shri-nanda-nandana" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Bhajahu̐ Re Mana <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Govinda Dās Kavirāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhajahu̐ re mana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-nanda-nandana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;abhaya-charaṇāravinda re</strong><br>
<strong>durlabha mānava&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;janama sat-saṅge</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;taraha e bhava-sindhu re</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O mind, serve the son of Nanda’s lotus feet, which make one fearless. Having attained this rare human birth, cross over the ocean of material existence by associating with sādhus.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śīta ātapa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vāta variṣaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;e dina yāminī jāgi re</strong><br>
<strong>viphale sevinu&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛpaṇa durajana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chapala sukha-lava lāgi’ re</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Day and night I remain awake, suffering from the heat and the cold, the wind and the rain. For a moment of flickering happiness I have uselessly served wicked and miserly men.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>e dhana, yauvana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;putra, parijana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ithe ki āchhe paratīti re</strong><br>
<strong>kamala-dala-jala&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jīvana ṭalamala</strong><br>
<strong>(ei āchhe ei nāi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jīvana ṭalamala)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhajahu̐ hari-pada niti re</strong></p>
<p class="translation">What is there in having wealth, youth, sons, and family? Life is tottering like water on a lotus petal (it is here, and then it is gone). O mind, serve the Lord’s feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
