<p class="verse"><strong>śravaṇa, kīrtana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;smaraṇa, vandana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pāda-sevana, dāsya re</strong><br>
<strong>pūjana, sakhī-jana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ātma-nivedana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;govinda-dāsa-abhilāṣa re</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Govinda Dās desires to engage in the nine practices of devotion: hearing about, glorifying, remembering, praying to, attending to, serving, worshipping, befriending, and surrendering to the Lord.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-radha-madhava-kunja-bihari" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Jaya Rādhā-Mādhava <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>(jaya) rādhā-mādhava&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(jaya) kuñja-vihārī</strong><br>
<strong>(jaya) gopī-jana-vallabha&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(jaya) giri-vara-dhārī</strong><br>
<strong>(jaya) yaśodā-nandana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(jaya) vraja-jana-rañjana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(jaya) yāmuna-tīra-vana-chārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to Śrī Rādhā’s Mādhava, who sports in the groves of Vraja! All glory to the gopīs’ beloved, the lifter of Govardhan Hill! All glory to Yaśodā’s darling son, the delight of Vraja’s residents! All glory to He who plays in the forests along the banks of the Yamunā!</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jaya-yashoda-nandana-krishna-gopala-govinda" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Jaya Yaśodā-Nandana Kṛṣṇa <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>

<p class="verse"><strong>(jaya) yaśodā-nandana kṛṣṇa gopāla govinda</strong><br>
<strong>(jaya) madana-mohana hari ananta mukunda</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to Yaśodā’s darling son, Kṛṣṇa, who protects and pleases the cows! All glory to the enchanter of Cupid, the infinite Lord, who takes away all inauspiciousness and grants liberation!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>(jaya) achyuta mādhava rāma vṛndāvana-chandra</strong><br>
<strong>(jaya) muralī-vadana śyāma gopī-janānanda</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to the infallible Lord, the moon of Vṛndāvan, who pleases Śrī Rādhā! All glory to the dark Flute Player, the ecstasy of the gopīs!</p>
</div>

<section class="song" id="song-mayura-mukuta-pitambara-dhari" data-translated="1">

<p class="translation">All glory to the flute player, the lifter of Govardhan Hill, Kṛṣṇa, Murāri, Yaśodā’s darling son!</p>

<section class="song" id="song-he-deva-bhavantam-vande" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>He deva bhavantaṁ vande <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī Prabhu</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>he deva bhavantaṁ vande</strong><br>
<strong>man-mānasa-madhukaram arpaya nija-pada-</strong><br>
<strong>paṅkaja-makarande</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord, I offer my obeisance unto You. Please place the bee of my heart in the honey of Your lotus feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yadapi samādhiṣu vidhir api paśyati</strong><br>
<strong>na tava nakhā gramarīchim</strong><br>
<strong>idam ichāmi niśamya tavāchyuta</strong><br>
<strong>tad api kṛpādbhuta vīchim</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Achyuta! Although even Brahmā, in samādhi, cannot see the lustre of the tips of Your toenails, I have heard of the waves of Your wonderful mercy and I desire to see it.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhaktir udañchati yad api mādhava</strong><br>
<strong>na tvayi mama tila-mātrī</strong><br>
<strong>parameśvaratā tad api tavādhika-</strong><br>
<strong>durghaṭa-ghaṭana-vidhātrī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Mādhava! Although I don’t have even a sesame seed of devotion to You, Your supreme power is the creator of even more improbable miracles.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ayam avilolatayādya sanātana</strong><br>
<strong>kalitādbhuta-rasa-bhāram</strong><br>
<strong>nivasatu nityam ihāmṛta-nindini</strong><br>
<strong>vindan madhurima-sāram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O eternal Lord, may the bee of my heart steadily reside eternally at Your lotus feet, where there is an abundance of the most wonderful honey—which surpasses the nectar of immortality—and thus attain the ultimate sweetness.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-janama-saphala-tara-krishna-darashiana-jara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Rūpa-Kīrtan <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>janama saphala tā’ra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa-daraśana yā’ra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhāgye ha-iyāchhe eka-bāra</strong><br>
<strong>vikaśiyā hṛn-nayana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kari’ kṛṣṇa daraśana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chhāḍe jīva chitera vikāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The birth of one who, by good fortune, has once seen Kṛṣṇa is successful. Developing the heart’s eyes and seeing Kṛṣṇa, such a soul abandons all delusions.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vṛndāvana-keli chatur vanamālī</strong><br>
<strong>tribhaṅga-bhaṅgimā-rūpa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vaṁśī-dhārī aparūpa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rasamaya nidhi, guṇa-śālī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Kṛṣṇa is expert at play in Vṛndāvan. He wears a forest garland, poses bent in three places, and plays the flute perfectly. He is a reservoir of rasa and possesses all qualities.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>varṇa nava jaladhara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śire śikhi-pichchha-vara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;alakā tilaka śobhā pāya</strong><br>
<strong>paridhāne pīta-vāsa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vadane madhura hāsa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hena rūpa jagat mātāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">His complexion is the colour of a fresh rain cloud. His forehead is adorned with tilak, curling locks, and a fine peacock feather. He wears yellow garments and a sweet smile on His face. His form enchants the world.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>indra-nīla jini&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa-rūpa-khāni</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;heriyā kadamba-mūle</strong><br>
<strong>mana uchāṭana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā chale charaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṁsāra gelāma bhule</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Beholding Kṛṣṇa’s form, which surpasses the beauty of a sapphire, beneath a kadamba, my heart was overwhelmed. My feet could not move. I forgot the world.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(sakhī he) sudhāmaya se rūpa mādhurī</strong><br>
<strong>dekhile nayana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;haya achetana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jhare premamaya vāri</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O friend! When I saw the beauty of Kṛṣṇa’s nectarean form, I fainted and shed tears of divine love.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kibā chūḍā śire&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kibā vaṁśī kare</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kibā se tribhaṅga ṭhāma</strong><br>
<strong>charaṇa-kamale&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;amiyā uchhale</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhāte nūpura dāma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">What a crown on His head! What a flute in His hand! What a threefold pose! Nectar surged from His lotus feet, which were adorned with tinkling anklets.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sadā āśā kari&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhṛṅga-rūpa dhari</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;charaṇa-kamale sthāna</strong><br>
<strong>anāyāse pāi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa-guṇa gāi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āra nā bhajiba āna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I eternally desire to take the form of a bee, easily attain a place at Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet, and sing His glories. I will never serve anyone else.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jaya-jaya-radha-krishna-jugala-milan" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Vraja-dhāma-mahimāmṛta <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Kṛṣṇadās Kavirāj Goswāmī</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya rādhe, jaya kṛṣṇa, jaya vṛndāvana</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-govinda, gopīnātha, madana-mohana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to Rādhā, Kṛṣṇa, and Vṛndāvan! All glory to Śrī Govinda, Gopīnāth, and Madan Mohan!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śyāma-kuṇḍa, rādhā-kuṇḍa, giri-govardhana</strong><br>
<strong>kālindī yamunā jaya, jaya mahāvana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to Śyām Kuṇḍa, Rādhā Kuṇḍa, Govardhan Hill, the River Yamunā, and Mahāvan!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>keśī-ghāṭa, vaṁśī-vaṭa, dvādaśa-kānana</strong><br>
<strong>yā̐hā saba līlā kaila śrī-nanda-nandana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to Keśī Ghāṭ, Vaṁśī Vaṭ, and Vraja’s twelve forests! Nanda’s son performed His Pastimes in all of these places.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-nanda-yaśodā jaya, jaya gopa-gaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>śrīdāmādi jaya, jaya dhenu-vatsa-gaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to Śrī Nanda and Yaśodā! All glory to Śrīdām and all the cowherd boys! All glory to Vraja’s cows and calves!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya vṛṣabhānu, jaya kīrtidā sundarī</strong><br>
<strong>jaya paurṇamāsī, jaya ābhīra-nāgarī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to Vṛṣabhānu and beautiful Kīrtidā! All glory to Paurṇamāsī! All glory to Vraja’s cowherd girls!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya jaya gopeśvara vṛndāvana-mājha</strong><br>
<strong>jaya jaya kṛṣṇa-sakhā baṭu dvija-rāja</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to Gopeśvar Śiva in Vṛndāvan! All glory to Kṛṣṇa’s funny brāhmaṇ friend, Madhumaṅgal!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya rāma-ghāṭa, jaya rohiṇī-nandana</strong><br>
<strong>jaya jaya vṛndāvana-vāsī yata jana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to Rām Ghāṭ! All glory to Rohiṇī’s son Balarām! All glory to all of Vṛndāvan’s residents!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya dvija-patnī, jaya nāga-kanyā-gaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>bhaktite yā̐hārā pāila govinda-charaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to the brāhmaṇ’s wives and Kālīya’s wives! Through their devotion, they attained Govinda’s feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-rāsa-maṇḍala jaya, jaya rādhā-śyāma</strong><br>
<strong>jaya jaya rāsa-līlā sarva-manorama</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to the site of the rāsa dance! All glory to Rādhā and Śyām! All glory to the rāsa dance, which captures the hearts of all!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya jayojjvala-rasa sarva-rasa-sāra</strong><br>
<strong>parakīyā-bhāve yāhā vrajete prachāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to madhura-rasa, the best of all rasas, which is manifest in Vraja as paramour love!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-jāhnavā-pāda-padma kariyā smaraṇa</strong><br>
<strong>dīna kṛṣṇa-dāsa kahe nāma-saṅkīrtana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Remembering the lotus feet of Śrī Jāhnavā Devī, the humble Kṛṣṇa Dās chants the Holy Name.</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section class="category">
<h1>Prayers</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-amara-jivana-sada-pape-rata" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Āmāra jīvana <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āmāra jīvana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā pāpe rata</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāhika puṇyera leśa</strong><br>
<strong>parere udvega&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;diyāchhi ye kata</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;diyāchhi jīvere kleśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">My life is always engrossed in sin. There is not a trace of piety within it. I have caused others so much anxiety and trouble.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nija sukha lāgi’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pāpe nāhi ḍari</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dayā-hīna svārtha-para</strong><br>
<strong>para-sukhe duḥkhī&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā mithya-bhāṣī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;para-duḥkha sukha-kara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I am never afraid to engage in sinful activities for the sake of my own happiness. I am merciless and selfish. I am a perpetual liar. I am saddened by the happiness of others and pleased by the sadness of others.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>aśeṣa kāmanā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hṛdi mājhe mora</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;krodhī, dambha-parāyaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>mada-matta sadā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viṣaye mohita</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hiṁsā-garva vibhūṣana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">There are unlimited sinful desires within the core of my heart. I am angry and arrogant, always maddened with vanity, infatuated with mundanity, and ornamented with malice and pride.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nidrālasya hata&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sukārye virata</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;akārye udyogī āmi</strong><br>
<strong>pratiṣṭhā lāgiyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śāṭhya-ācharaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;lobha-hata sadā kāmī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I am subdued by laziness and sleep, averse to performing good deeds, and eager to perform sinful deeds. I engage in deceitful behaviour in pursuit of prestige. I am ruined by greed and constantly covetous.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>e hena durjana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sajjana-varjita</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;aparādhi nirantara</strong><br>
<strong>śubha-kārya-śūnya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadānartha-manāḥ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nānā duḥkhe jara jara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Such a sinner as I is shunned by devotees, and is a constant offender. I am devoid of virtuous motives, always intent upon evil, and racked by various miseries.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vārdhakye ekhana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;upāya-vihīna</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā’te dīna akiñchana</strong><br>
<strong>bhakati-vinoda&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prabhura charaṇe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kare duḥkha nivedana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Now aged, I am devoid of any means of deliverance and thus destitute and distressed. Bhakti Vinod submits this remorseful confession at the Lord’s feet.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-hari-hari-biphole-janama-goyainu" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Iṣṭa-deve Vijñapti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari hari!</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viphale janama goṅāinu</strong><br>
<strong>manuṣya-janama pāiyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rādhā-kṛṣṇa nā bhajiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jāniyā śuniyā viṣa khāinu</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord! My life has passed uselessly. I attained a human birth, but I did not serve Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa. Knowingly I drank poison.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>golokera prema-dhana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hari-nāma-saṅkīrtana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rati nā janmila kena tāya</strong><br>
<strong>saṁsāra-viṣānale&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;divā-niśi hiyā jvale</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;juḍāite nā kainu upāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Why didn’t I develop attachment to Hari-nām-saṅkīrtan, to Goloka’s divine love and wealth? My heart burns day and night in the poisonous fire of saṁsāra, yet I have not sought any way to relieve it.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vrajendra-nandana yei&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śachī-suta haila sei</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;balarāma ha-ila nitāi</strong><br>
<strong>dīna-hīna yata chhila&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hari-nāme uddhārila</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāra śākṣī jagāi mādhāi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Vrajendra Nandan became Śachī Suta and Balarām became Nitāi. They delivered all the fallen souls with the Holy Name. Jagāi and Mādhāi are evidence of this.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hā hā prabhu nanda-suta&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vṛṣabhānu-sutā-yuta</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;karuṇā karaha ei-bāra</strong><br>
<strong>narottama-dāsa kaya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā ṭheliha rāṅgā pāya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomā vine ke āchhe āmāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Rādhā! O Lord Kṛṣṇa! Be merciful to me this time. Narottam Dās says, “Please don’t push me away from Your reddish feet. Without You, who do I have?”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-ki-jani-ki-bale-tomara-dhamete" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Goptṛtve Varaṇa <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ki jāni ki bale&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra dhāmete</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ha-inu śaraṇāgata</strong><br>
<strong>tumi dayāmaya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;patita-pāvana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;patita-tāraṇe rata</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I do not know by what force I have taken shelter within Your abode. Surely it is by Your mercy alone, for You are ever seeking the purification and deliverance of the fallen souls.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bharasā āmāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ei mātra nātha!</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tumi ta’ karuṇā-maya</strong><br>
<strong>tava dayā pātra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāhi mora sama</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;avaśya ghuchāre bhaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord! You are merciful. This is my sole hope. There is no one who needs Your mercy more than I. Certainly You will dispel all my fear.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āmāre tārite&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kāhāra śakati</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;avanī-bhitare nāhi</strong><br>
<strong>dayāla ṭhākura!&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ghoṣaṇā tomāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;adhama pāmare trāhi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">There is no one on earth who has the power to deliver me. O merciful Lord! It is Your declaration that You deliver the lowest, most sinful persons.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sakala chhāḍiyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āsiyāchhi āmi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra charaṇe nātha!</strong><br>
<strong>āmi nitya-dāsa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tumi pālayitā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tumi goptā, jagannātha!</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Abandoning everything, I have come to Your feet, O Lord! I am Your eternal servant, and You are my maintainer and protector, O Lord of the universe!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra sakala&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āmi mātra dāsa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āmāra tāribe tumi</strong><br>
<strong>tomāra charaṇa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;karinu varaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āmāra nahi ta’ āmi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Everything is Yours. I am only a servant. Certainly You will deliver me. I have embraced Your feet and no longer belong to myself.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakati-vinoda&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kā̐diya śaraṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;la’yechhe tomāra pāya</strong><br>
<strong>kṣami’ aparādha&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāme ruchi diyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pālana karahe tāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Weeping, Bhakti Vinod takes shelter at Your feet. Forgiving his offences and giving him a taste for the Name, kindly maintain him.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-sarvasva-tomara-charane-sanpiya" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Sarvasva tomāra <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sarvasva tomāra,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;charaṇe sa̐piyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;paḍechhi tomāra ghare</strong><br>
<strong>tumi ta’ ṭhākura,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra kukkura,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;baliyā jānaha more</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have offered everything at Your feet and taken shelter in Your home. You are my Master. Consider me Your dog.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bā̐dhiyā nikaṭe,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āmāre pālibe,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rahiba tomāra dvāre</strong><br>
<strong>pratīpa-janere,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āsite nā diba,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rākhiba gaḍera pāre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You will tie me up nearby and maintain me. I will remain at Your doorstep. I will not allow any inimical persons to enter; I will keep them on the far side of Your moat.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tava nija-jana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prasāda seviyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;uchchhiṣṭa rākhibe yāhā</strong><br>
<strong>āmāra bhojana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;parama-ānande,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prati-dina habe tāhā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Whatever remnants Your devotees leave behind after honouring Your prasād I will eat every day with great joy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>basiyā śuiyā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra charaṇa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chintiba satata āmi</strong><br>
<strong>nāchite nāchite,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nikaṭe yāiba,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yakhana ḍākibe tumi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">While sitting up or lying down, I will always think of Your feet. I will come to You whenever You call me, dancing all the way.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nijera poṣana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kabhu nā bhāviba,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rahiba bhāvera bhare</strong><br>
<strong>bhakati-vinoda,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāre pālaka,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;baliyā varaṇa kare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I will never think about my own maintenance; I will always remain within the shelter of loving attachment to You. Bhakti Vinod embraces You as his maintainer.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-atma-nivedana-tuya-pade-kori" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Ātma-nivedana <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ātma-nivedana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tuyā pade kari</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ha-inu parama sukhī</strong><br>
<strong>duḥkha dūre gela&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chintā nā rahila</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chaudike ānanda dekhi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Surrendering myself at Your feet, I have become completely happy. All my distress has gone away, I have no more anxiety, and I see joy in all directions.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>aśoka-abhaya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;amṛta-ādhāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra charaṇa-dvaya</strong><br>
<strong>tāhāte ekhana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viśrāma labhiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chhāḍinu bhavera bhaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Your feet are the abode of sorrowlessness, fearlessness, and nectar. Receiving shelter beneath them, I have abandoned all fear of material existence.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra saṁsāre&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kariba sevana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nahiba phalera bhāgī</strong><br>
<strong>tava sukha yāhe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kariba yatana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ha’ye pade anurāgī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I will serve in Your home and will not become an enjoyer of the results. Lovingly attached to Your feet, I will carefully do whatever pleases You.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra sevāya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;duḥkha haya yata</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;seo ta’ parama sukha</strong><br>
<strong>sevā-sukha-duḥkha&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;parama sampada</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāśaye avidyā-duḥkha</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All the distress I encounter in Your service is also my great happiness. Both the distress and the happiness that come from serving You are my great fortune. They both destroy the distress of ignorance.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pūrva itihāsa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhulinu sakala</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sevā-sukha pe’ye mane</strong><br>
<strong>āmi ta’ tomāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tumi ta’ āmāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ki kāja apara dhane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Feeling the happiness of Your service in my heart, I have completely forgotten all past history. I am Yours, and You are mine. What need is there for any other wealth?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakati-vinoda&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ānande ḍubiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra sevāra tare</strong><br>
<strong>saba cheṣṭā kare&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tava ichhā-mata</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;thākiyā tomāra ghare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Diving in the joy of Your service, Bhakti Vinod resides within Your home and performs all his endeavours in accordance with Your will.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-gauranga-bolite-hobe-pulaka-shorira" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>‘Gaurāṅga’ bolite habe <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>‘gaurāṅga’ balite habe pulaka śarīra</strong><br>
<strong>‘hari hari’ balite nayane va’be nīra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will the hairs on my body stand on end as I chant “Gaurāṅga!”? When will tears pour from my eyes as I chant, “Hari! Hari!”?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āra kabe nitāichā̐da karuṇā karibe</strong><br>
<strong>saṁsāra-vāsanā more kabe tuchchha ha’be</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will Nītāichā̐d be merciful to me? When will my worldly desires become insignificant?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>viṣaya chhāḍiyā kabe śuddha ha’be mana</strong><br>
<strong>kabe hāma heraba śrī-vṛndāvana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will my mind give up all worldliness and become pure? When will I behold Śrī Vṛndāvan?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rūpa-raghunātha-pade ha-ibe ākuti</strong><br>
<strong>kabe hāma bujhaba se yugala-pīriti</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will I long for Śrī Rūpa and Śrī Raghunāth’s feet? When will I understand the Divine Couple’s love?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rūpa-raghunātha-pade rahu mora āśa</strong><br>
<strong>prārthanā karaye sadā narottama dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“May my aspiration be at the feet of Śrī Rūpa and Śrī pkobe-hobe-bolo-se-dina-amarRaghunāth.” Narottam Dās always prays in this way.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-kobe-hobe-bolo-se-dina-amar" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Kabe ha’be bala <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kabe ha’be bala se-dina āmāra</strong><br>
<strong>(āmār) aparādha ghuchi’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śuddha nāme ruchi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛpā-bale ha’be hṛdaye sañchāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Please tell me, when will that day be mine when my offences will be dispelled and taste for the pure Name will be infused within my heart by divine grace?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tṛṇādhika hīna&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kabe nije māni</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sahiṣṇutā-guṇa hṛdayete āni’</strong><br>
<strong>sakale mānada&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āpani amānī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;haye āsvādiba nāma-rasa-sāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will I feel myself to be lower than a blade of grass, bring the quality of tolerance within my heart, become eager to honour everyone, and, devoid of desire for honour from anyone, relish the essence of the Name’s rasa?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dhana jana āra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kavitā, sundarī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;baliba nā chāhi deha-sukha-karī</strong><br>
<strong>janme-janme dāo&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ohe gaurahari!</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ahaitukī-bhakti charaṇe tomāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will I declare that I do not desire wealth, followers, erudition, beautiful women, or any other bodily pleasures? O Gaurahari! Birth after birth, please give me unconditional devotion to Your feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>(kabe) karite śrī-kṛṣṇa-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāma uchāraṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pulakita deha gadgada vachana</strong><br>
<strong>vaivarṇya-vepathu&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;habe saṅghaṭana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nirantara netre va’be aśru-dhāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will my hair stand on end, my voice falter, my complexion turn pale, my body tremble, and tears pour incessantly from my eyes while I call aloud Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s Name?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kabe navadvīpe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;suradhunī-taṭe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘gaura-nityānanda’ bali’ niṣkapaṭe</strong><br>
<strong>nāchiyā gāiyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;beḍāiba chhuṭe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vātulera prāya chhāḍiyā vichāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When, in the land of Nabadwīp, on the banks of the Ganges, will I run about, guilelessly calling, “O Gaura! O Nityānanda!”, dancing and singing like a madman, giving up all considerations?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kabe nityānanda&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;more kari’ dayā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chhāḍāibe mora viṣayera māyā</strong><br>
<strong>diyā more nija-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;charaṇera chhāyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāmera hāṭete dibe adhikāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will Nityānanda be merciful to me and cause me to abandon my attachment to mundanity? When will He give me the shade of His feet and grant me a place in the marketplace of the Name?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kiniba, luṭiba&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hari-nāma-rasa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāma-rase māti’ ha-iba vivaśa</strong><br>
<strong>rasera rasika-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;charaṇa paraśa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kariyā majiba rase anibāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will I buy and festively scatter the Name’s rasa? When will I become intoxicated and overwhelmed with that rasa? When will I touch the feet of a relisher of that rasa and become immersed in that rasa perpetually?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kabe jīve dayā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ha-ibe udaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nija-sukha bhuli’ sudīna-hṛdaya</strong><br>
<strong>bhakati-vinoda&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kariyā vinaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-ājñā-ṭahala karibe prachāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will compassion for all souls arise within me? When will Bhakti Vinod forget his own happiness and travel about respectfully preaching Śrī Gaurasundar’s teachings with a most humble heart?</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-kobe-gaura-vane-suradhuni-tate" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Siddhi-Lālasā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kabe gaura-vane&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;suradhunī-taṭe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘hā rādhe hā kṛṣṇa’ ba’le</strong><br>
<strong>kā̐diyā beḍāba&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;deha-sukha chhāḍi’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nānā latā-taru-tale</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will I abandon all bodily pleasures and wander along the banks of the Ganges beneath the trees and creepers in Śrī Gaurasundar’s forests, crying and calling out, “O Rādhā! O Kṛṣṇa!”?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śva-pacha-gṛhete&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;māgiyā khāiba</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;piba sarasvatī-jala</strong><br>
<strong>puline puline&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gaḍāgaḍi diba</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kari’ kṛṣṇa-kolāhala</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will I eat by begging food from the houses of dogeating outcasts and drink the water of the river Saraswatī? When will I roll about on the banks of the Ganges and uproariously call out, “Kṛṣṇa!”?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dhāma-vāsī jane&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;praṇati kariyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;māgiba kṛpāra leśa</strong><br>
<strong>vaiṣṇava-charaṇa-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;reṇu gāya mākhi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dhari’ avadhūta-veśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will I bow down before the residents of Śrī Gaurasundar’s abode and beg for a particle of their mercy? When will I wear the garb of an avadhūt and smear my body with the dust of the devotees’ feet?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gauḍa-vraja-vane&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bheda nā heriba</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ha-iba varaja-vāsī</strong><br>
<strong>dhāmera svarūpa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sphuribe nayane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ha-iba rādhāra dāsī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will I see no difference between the residents of Gauḍa and the residents of Vraja, and become a resident of Vraja? When will the true nature of the Lord’s abode manifest before my eyes? When will I become Śrī Rādhā’s maidservant?</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section class="category">
<h1>Advice</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-durlabha-manava-janma-labhiya-samsare" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Durlabha mānava-janma <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>durlabha mānava-janma labhiyā saṁsāre</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa nā bhajinu duḥkha kahiba kāhāre?</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I attained a human birth, which is most rare in this world, but did not serve Kṛṣṇa. To whom shall I express my sorrow?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>‘saṁsāra’ ‘saṁsāra’ kari’ michhe gela kāla</strong><br>
<strong>lābha nā ha-ila kichhu, ghaṭila jañjāla</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Living life after life in this mundane world, my time has passed in vain. I have not gained anything. I have only suffered.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kisera saṁsāra ei chhāyābāji prāya</strong><br>
<strong>ihāte mamatā kari vṛthā dina yāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">What sort of world is this? It is like a shadow play. Attaching myself to it, my days pass by in vain.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>e deha patana ha’le ki rabe āmāra?</strong><br>
<strong>keha sukha nāhi dibe putra paribāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When this body drops dead, what will remain ‘mine’? Upon death neither my sons nor my family will give me any satisfaction.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gardabhera mata āmi kari pariśrama</strong><br>
<strong>kāra lāgi’ eta kari, nā ghuchila bhrama</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I toil laboriously like an ass. For whom am I doing this? Still my illusion has not been dispelled.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dina yāya michhā kāje, niśā nidrā-vaśe</strong><br>
<strong>nāhi bhāvi maraṇa nikaṭe āchhe vase</strong></p>
<p class="translation">My days pass in meaningless labour, and my nights pass in sleep. I never consider that death is sitting nearby.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhāla manda khāi, heri, pari, chintā-hīna</strong><br>
<strong>nāhi bhāvi, e deha chhāḍiba kona dina</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I eat luxuriously, look around, and dress carelessly, never considering that one day I will leave this body.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>deha-geha-kalatrādi-chintā avirata</strong><br>
<strong>jāgichhe hṛdaye mora buddhi kari hata</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Constant thoughts of my body, home, wife, and so on, arise in my heart and spoil my intelligence.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hāya, hāya! nāhi bhāvi—anitya e saba</strong><br>
<strong>jīvana vigate kothā rahibe vaibhava?</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Alas! Alas! I never considered that all of these things are temporary. When my soul departs, where will all these opulences remain?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śmaśāne śarīra mama paḍiyā rahibe</strong><br>
<strong>vihaṅga-pataṅga tāya vihāra karibe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">My body will be laid to rest at the crematorium. There birds and insects will enjoy it.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kukkura sṛgāla saba ānandita ha’ye</strong><br>
<strong>mahotsava karibe āmāra deha la’ye</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Dogs and jackals will happily have a festival with my body.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ye dehera ei gati, tāra anugata</strong><br>
<strong>saṁsāra-vaibhava āra bandhu-jana yata</strong></p>
<p class="translation">This is the fate of my body, its effects, material wealth, and all of my companions.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ataeva māyā-moha chhāḍi buddhimāna</strong><br>
<strong>nitya-tattva kṛṣṇa-bhakti karuna sandhāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">So, intelligent souls! Abandon Māyā’s illusion and search for the eternal truth: devotion to Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-e-ghora-samsare-podiya-manava" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>E ghora saṁsāre <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>e ghora-saṁsāre&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;paḍiyā mānava</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā pāya duḥkhera śeṣa</strong><br>
<strong>sādhu-saṅga kari’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hari bhaje yadi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tabe anta haya kleśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">A person who has fallen into this terrible world never ceases to suffer. If one associates with sādhus and serves Hari, however, one’s suffering comes to an end.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>viṣaya anale&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jvalichhe hṛdaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;anale bāḍaye anala</strong><br>
<strong>aparādha chhāḍi’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;laya kṛṣṇa-nāma</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;anale paḍaye jala</strong></p>
<p class="translation">A fallen soul burns in the ever-increasing fire of mundanity. Avoiding offences, chant Kṛṣṇa’s Name. This pours water on the fire.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nitāi chaitanya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;charaṇa-kamale</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āśraya la-ila yei</strong><br>
<strong>kāli dāsa bale&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jīvane maraṇe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āmāra āśraya sei</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Kāli Dās says, “Anyone who has taken shelter of Nitāi and Chaitanya’s lotus feet is my shelter, in life and in death.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-haribolo-haribolo-haribolo-bhai-re" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Sri Nama Kirtan Song 3 <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>haribolo haribolo haribolo bhāi re</strong><br>
<strong>hari-nāma aniyāchhe gauraṅga-nitāi re</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(modera duḥkha dekhe re)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Brothers! Chant “Hari”! Chant “Hari”! Chant “Hari”! Gaurāṅga and Nitāi have brought the Lord’s Name. (Oh! They see our suffering.)</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari-nāma vinā jīvera anya dhana nāi re</strong><br>
<strong>hari-nāme śuddha ha’la jagāi-mādhāi re</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(baḍa pāpī chhila re)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The soul has no wealth other than the Lord’s Name. Oh! Jagāi and Mādhāi were purified by the Lord’s Name (Oh! They were great sinners).</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>michhe māyā-baddha ha’ye jīvana kāṭāi re</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(āmi āmāra bale re)</strong><br>
<strong>āśā-vaśe ghure’ ghure’ āra kothā yāi re</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(āśāra śeṣa nāi re)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bound by Māyā (claiming ‘I’ and ‘mine’), I pass my life in vain. Oh! Controlled by mundane desires, wandering and wandering, where shall I go next? (Oh! Mundane desires never end).</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari bale deo bhāi āśāra mukhe chhāi re</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(nirāśa ta’ sukha re)</strong><br>
<strong>bhoga-mokṣa-vāñchhā chhāḍi’ hari-nāma gāi re</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(śuddha-sattva ha’ye re)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O brothers! Chant “Hari!” and throw ashes in the face of material desire. (Oh! Being desireless is happiness). Abandoning desire for mundane enjoyment and liberation, I chant the Lord’s Name (being spiritually pure).</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nā cheye-o nāmera guṇe o saba phala pāi re</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(tuchchha phaler prayāsa chheḍe re)</strong><br>
<strong>vinoda bale yāi la’ye nāmera bālāi re</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(nāmera bālāi chheḍe re)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Even without desiring them, I obtain all ends (dharma, artha, kāma, and mokṣa) by the Name’s power. (Oh! Abandon all endeavours for insignificant ends). Bhakti Vinod says, “I proceed, removing the obstacles to the Lord’s Name. (Oh! Avoid the obstacles to the Lord’s Name.)”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-hari-nama-mahamantra-sarva-mantra-sara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Daśa-vidha Nāmāparādha <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari-nāma mahāmantra sarva-mantra-sāra</strong><br>
<strong>yā̐dera karuṇā-bale jagate prachāra</strong><br>
<strong>sei nāma-parāyaṇa sādhu, mahājana</strong><br>
<strong>tā̐hādera nindā nā kariha kadāchana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The Hari-nām mahāmantra is the best of all mantras and is distributed throughout the world by the mercy of the sādhus devoted to the Name. Never criticise such great souls.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vrajendra-nandana kṛṣṇa sarveśvareśvara</strong><br>
<strong>maheśvara ādi tā̐ra sevana-tatpara</strong><br>
<strong>nāma chintāmaṇi kṛṣṇa-chaitanya-svarūpa</strong><br>
<strong>bheda-jñāna nā karibe līlā-guṇa-rūpa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Vrajendra Nandan Kṛṣṇa is the Lord of all Lords. Śiva and all other gods are dedicated to His service. The Name is a wish-fulfilling jewel and a spiritual form of Kṛṣṇa Himself. Do not consider Kṛṣṇa’s Name to be distinct from His Form, Qualities, or Pastimes.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>“guru kṛṣṇa-rūpa hana śāstrera pramāṇe</strong><br>
<strong>guru-rūpe kṛṣṇa kṛpā kare bhāgyavāne”</strong><br>
<strong>se gurute martya-buddhi avajñādi tyaji</strong><br>
<strong>iṣṭa-lābha kara, nirantara nāma bhaji</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“The scriptures confirm that Guru is a form of Kṛṣṇa (SB: 11.17.27). In the form of Guru, Kṛṣṇa bestows His mercy upon the fortunate.” Give up the conception that Guru is a mortal, and all other types of disrespect, and attain your cherished goal: serving the Name eternally.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śruti, śruti-mātā-saha sātvata purāṇa</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-nāma-charaṇa-padma kare nīrājana</strong><br>
<strong>sei śruti-śāstra yebā karaye nindana</strong><br>
<strong>se aparadhīra saṅga karibe varjana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The Vedas, their mother, Gāyatrī, and <i>Śrīmad Bhāgavatam</i> worship the Holy Name’s lotus feet. Reject the association of any offender who criticises these scriptures.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nāmera mahimā sarva-śāstrete vākhāne</strong><br>
<strong>atistuti, hena kabhu nā bhāviha mane</strong><br>
<strong>agastya, ananta, brahmā, śivādi satata</strong><br>
<strong>ye nāma-mahimā-gāthā saṅkīrtana-rata</strong><br>
<strong>se nāma-mahimā-sindhu ke pāibe pāra?</strong><br>
<strong>atistuti bale yei—sei durāchāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The Name’s glories are proclaimed in all the scriptures. Never consider such glorification to be exaggerated praise. Agastya, Ananta, Brahmā, Śiva, and so on are always devoted to chanting the Name’s glories. Who can cross the ocean of those glories? Anyone who considers them exaggerated praise is wicked.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛṣṇa-nāmāvali nitya golokera dhana</strong><br>
<strong>kalpita, prākṛta, bhāve—aparādhi-jana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Kṛṣṇa’s Names are the eternal wealth of Goloka. Anyone who considers Them imaginary or mundane is an offender.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nāme sarva-pāpa-kṣaya sarva-śāstre kaya</strong><br>
<strong>sārā-dina pāpa kari sei bharasāya—</strong><br>
<strong>emata durbuddhi yāra sei aparādhī</strong><br>
<strong>māyā-pravañchita, duḥkha bhuñje niravadhi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All the scriptures declare that the Name destroys all sin. The wicked who sin all day long in expectation of this are offenders. They are deluded by māyā and suffer perpetually.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>atulya śrī-kṛṣṇa-nāma pūrṇa-rasa-nidhi</strong><br>
<strong>tā̐ra sama nā bhāviha śubha-karma ādi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s incomparable Name is a brimming reservoir of rasa. Chanting the Name should never be considered equal to pious worldly action (such as adhering to vows, practising renunciation, performing austerities, making sacrificial offerings, and so on).</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nāme śraddhā-hina-jana—vidhātā vāñchita</strong><br>
<strong>tāre nāma dāne aparādha suniśchita</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Those who have no faith in the Name have been deceived by Providence. To give the Name to them is certainly an offence.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śuniyāo kṛṣṇa-nāma-māhātmya apāra</strong><br>
<strong>ye prīti-rahita, sei narādhama chhāra</strong><br>
<strong>ahaṁtā mamatā yāra antare bāhire</strong><br>
<strong>śuddha kṛṣṇa-nāma tāra kabhu nāhi sphure</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Those who remain devoid of love for Kṛṣṇa’s Name even after hearing His boundless glories are deplorable, fallen souls. The pure Name of Kṛṣṇa never reveals Himself to those who are internally and externally ridden with egotism and possessiveness.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ei daśa aparādha kariyā varjjana</strong><br>
<strong>ye sujana kare harināma saṅkīrtana</strong><br>
<strong>apūrva śrī-kṛṣṇa-prema labhya tāre haya</strong><br>
<strong>nāma-prabhu tāra hṛde nitya vilasaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Great souls who avoid these ten offences and engage in Hari-nām-saṅkīrtan attain unprecedented Śrī Kṛṣṇa-prema. The Name Himself plays within their hearts eternally.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-prabhu-kohe-kohilan-ei-mahamantra" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Hare Kṛṣṇa mahāmantra <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Vṛndāvan Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>hare kṛṣṇa hare kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa hare hare</strong><br>
<strong>hare rāma hare rāma rāma rāma hare hare</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>prabhu kahe—kahilāṅ ei mahāmantra</strong><br>
<strong>ihā japa’ giyā sabe kariyā nirbandha</strong><br>
<strong>ihā haite sarva-siddhi ha-ibe sabāra</strong><br>
<strong>sarva-kṣaṇa bala’ ithe vidhi nāhi āra</strong><br>
<strong>ki śayane ki bhojane, ki vā jāgaraṇe</strong><br>
<strong>ahar-niśa chinta kṛṣṇa, balaha vadane</strong><br>
<strong>āma-prati sneha yadi thāke sabākāra</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa vinā keha kichhu nā balibe āra</strong></p>
<p class="translation"><i>(Śrī Chaitanya-bhāgavata: Madhya-khaṇḍa, 23.76–8, 28.27–8)</i><br>
Śrīman Mahāprabhu declared, “I have told you all this mahāmantra. Chant it earnestly. By doing so, you will attain all perfection. Chant always. There are no rules in that regard. Whether you are resting, eating, or awake—day and night think of Kṛṣṇa and chant His Name aloud. If you have any affection for Me, do not speak of anything other than Kṛṣṇa.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-cheto-darpana-marjanam" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>cheto-darpaṇa-mārjanaṁ bhava-mahā-dāvāgni-nirvāpaṇaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>śreyaḥ-kairava-chandrikā-vitaraṇaṁ vidyā-vadhū-jīvanam</strong><br>
<strong>ānandāmbudhi-vardhanaṁ prati-padaṁ pūrṇāmṛtāsvādanaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>sarvātma-snapanaṁ paraṁ vijayate śrī-kṛṣṇa-saṅkīrtanam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Kṛṣṇa-saṅkīrtan cleanses the mirror of consciousness, extinguishes the raging forest fire of material existence, shines moonlight on the evening lotus of good fortune, is the life of divine knowledge, expands the ocean of ecstasy, is the taste of full nectar at every moment, and soothes the entire self. May Śrī Kṛṣṇa-saṅkīrtan be supremely victorious!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>nāmnām akāri bahudhā nija-sarva-śaktis</strong><br>
<strong>tatrārpitā niyamitaḥ smaraṇe na kālaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>etādṛśī tava kṛpā bhagavan mamāpi</strong><br>
<strong>durdaivam īdṛśam ihājani nānurāgaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord, You have manifested Your numerous Names, placed all of Your power within Them, and made no rules regarding the time for remembering Them. Such is Your great mercy. My misfortune, however, is that I have no attachment to Your Names.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>tṛṇād api sunīchena taror iva sahiṣṇunā</strong><br>
<strong>amāninā mānadena kīrtanīyaḥ sadā hariḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The Lord’s Name is to be chanted always, with more humility than a blade of grass, tolerance like that of a tree, respect for everyone, and without desire for respect from anyone.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>na dhanaṁ na janaṁ na sundarīṁ</strong><br>
<strong>kavitāṁ vā jagad-īśa kāmaye</strong><br>
<strong>mama janmani janmanīśvare</strong><br>
<strong>bhavatād bhaktir ahaitukī tvayi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord of the universe, I do not desire wealth, followers, beautiful women, or erudition (dharma, artha, kāma, or mokṣa). Birth after birth, I desire only unconditional devotion to You.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>ayi nanda-tanuja kiṅkaraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>patitaṁ māṁ viṣame bhavāmbudhau</strong><br>
<strong>kṛpayā tava pāda-paṅkaja-</strong><br>
<strong>sthita-dhūlī-sadṛśaṁ vichintaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Kṛṣṇa, Your servant has fallen into the terrible ocean of this world. Mercifully consider me a speck of dust at Your lotus feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>nayanaṁ galad-aśru-dhārayā</strong><br>
<strong>vadanaṁ gadgada-ruddhayā girā</strong><br>
<strong>pulakair nichitaṁ vapuḥ kadā</strong><br>
<strong>tava nāma-grahaṇe bhaviṣyati</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will tears stream from my eyes, my voice falter, and the hair on my body stand on end as I call Your Name?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>yugāyitaṁ nimeṣeṇa chakṣuṣā prāvṛṣāyitam</strong><br>
<strong>śūnyāyitaṁ jagat sarvaṁ govinda-viraheṇa me</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Govinda, in separation from You, a moment feels like an age, tears pour from my eyes, and the world is void.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>āśliṣya vā pāda-ratāṁ pinaṣṭu mām</strong><br>
<strong>adarśanān marma-hatāṁ karotu vā</strong><br>
<strong>yathā tathā vā vidadhātu lampaṭo</strong><br>
<strong>mat-prāṇa-nāthas tu sa eva nāparaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">That debauchee may tightly embrace this maidservant, or He may leave me and break my heart. He may do as He likes, but He alone is the Lord of my heart.</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section class="category">
<h1>Songs by Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-sujanarvuda-radhita-pada-yugam" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Prabhupāda-padma Stavakaḥ <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sujanārvuda-rādhita-pāda-yugaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>yuga-dharma-dhurandhara-pātra-varam</strong><br>
<strong>varadābhaya-dāyaka-pūjya-padaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>praṇamāmi sadā prabhupāda-padam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">My master, Prabhupād Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Ṭhākur, established the religion of the age, Kṛṣṇa-saṅkīrtan, and is the chief minister of the universal assembly of leading Vaiṣṇavas. His lotus feet are worshipped by millions of virtuous souls and fulfil the desires of those who dispel souls’ fears. Eternally I offer my obeisance to my master’s worshippable feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhajanorjjita-sajjana-saṅgha-patiṁ</strong><br>
<strong>patitādhika-kāruṇikaika-gatim</strong><br>
<strong>gati-vañchita-vañchakāchintya-padaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>praṇamāmi sadā prabhupāda-padam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is the leader of the great souls who are endowed with devotion and the sole, greatly merciful shelter for the fallen. His inconceivable feet are the shelter of even the deceitful by deceiving them. Eternally I offer my obeisance to my master’s lotus feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>atikomala-kāñchana-dīrgha-tanuṁ</strong><br>
<strong>tanu-nindita-hema-mṛṇāla-madam</strong><br>
<strong>madanārvuda-vandita-chandra-padaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>praṇamāmi sadā prabhupāda-padam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">His long, supple, golden form crushes the pride of golden lotus stems. His moon-like feet are worshipped by millions of Cupids. Eternally I offer my obeisance to them.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nija-sevaka-tāraka-rañji-vidhuṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vidhutāhita-huṅkṛta-siṁha-varam</strong><br>
<strong>varaṇāgata-bāliśa-śanda-padaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>praṇamāmi sadā prabhupāda-padam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is the moon that delights his star-like servants. His great, lion-like roar scares away the inimical. His feet bestow auspiciousness upon the simple who accept them. Eternally I offer my obeisance to my master’s feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vipulīkṛta-vaibhava-gaura-bhuvaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhuvaneṣu vikīrtita-gaura-dayam</strong><br>
<strong>dayanīya-gaṇārpita-gaura-padaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>praṇamāmi sadā prabhupāda-padam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He reveals the divine glory of Śrī Gaura Dhām, propounds Śrī Gaura’s celebrated grace throughout the world, and installs Śrī Gaura within the hearts of the recipients of his grace. Eternally I offer my obeisance to my master’s feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chira-gaura-janāśraya-viśva-guruṁ</strong><br>
<strong>guru-gaura-kiśoraka-dāsya-param</strong><br>
<strong>paramādṛta-bhaktivinoda-padaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>praṇamāmi sadā prabhupāda-padam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is the eternal shelter of the souls surrendered unto Śrī Gaura and the Guru of the world. He is dedicated to the service of his Guru, Śrīla Gaura Kiśor, and completely devoted to Śrīla Bhakti Vinod. Eternally I offer my obeisance to my master’s feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>raghu-rūpa-sanātana-kīrtti-dharaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>dharaṇī-tala-kīrttita-jīva-kavim</strong><br>
<strong>kavirāja-narottama-sakhya-padaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>praṇamāmi sadā prabhupāda-padam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He expounds the glory of Śrī Rūpa, Śrī Sanātan, and Śrī Raghunāth. In this world he is glorified as being nondifferent from Śrī Jīva and a counterpart of Śrīla Kṛṣṇadās Kavirāj and Śrīla Narottam Ṭhākur. Eternally I offer my obeisance to my master’s feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛpayā hari-kīrtana-mūrti-dharaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>dharaṇī-bhara-hāraka-gaura-janam</strong><br>
<strong>janakādhika-vatsala-snigdha-padaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>praṇamāmi sadā prabhupāda-padam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is the embodiment of Hari-kīrtan. He mercifully relieves Mother Earth of the burden upon her. He is an associate of Śrī Gaura, and more kind and affectionate than a father. Eternally I offer my obeisance to my master’s feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śaraṇāgata-kiṅkara-kalpa-taruṁ</strong><br>
<strong>taru-dhik-kṛta dhīra vadānya varam</strong><br>
<strong>varadendra-gaṇārchita-divya-padaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>praṇamāmi sadā prabhupāda-padam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is a wish-fulfilling tree for the surrendered servants. His benevolence and tolerance shame even a tree’s. The demigods and sages worship his divine feet. Eternally I offer my obeisance to my master’s feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>parahaṁsa-varaṁ paramārtha-patiṁ</strong><br>
<strong>patitodharaṇe kṛta-veśa-yatim</strong><br>
<strong>yati-rāja-gaṇaiḥ parisevya-padaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>praṇamāmi sadā prabhupāda-padam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is the best of the paramahaṁsas and the guardian of the supreme wealth (Kṛṣṇa-prema). He accepted the robes of a sannyāsī to deliver the fallen. His feet are served by the best of the sannyāsīs. Eternally I offer my obeisance to my master’s feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vṛṣabhānu-sutā-dayitānucharaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>charaṇāśrita-reṇu-dharas tam aham</strong><br>
<strong>mahad-adbhuta-pāvana-śakti-padaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>praṇamāmi sadā prabhupāda-padam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī’s beloved servant. I hold on my head the dust of his tremendous, wondrously purifying feet and eternally offer my obeisance to them.</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>

<h5>11</h5>

<p class="verse"><strong>kṛṣṇa-dṛṣṭi-pāta-hetu-śabdakārtha-yojanaṁ</strong><br>
