<section class="category">
<h1>Holy Days</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-krishna-hoite-chatur-mukha" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Guru-paramparā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Verses 1-9 composed by Śrīla Saraswati Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛṣṇa haite chatur-mukha&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hana kṛṣṇa-sevonmukha</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;brahmā haite nāradera mati</strong><br>
<strong>nārada haite vyāsa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;madhva kahe vyāsa dāsa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pūrṇaprajña padmanābha gati</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By Kṛṣṇa’s association, Brahmā became devoted to Kṛṣṇa’s service. By Brahmā’s association, Nārad Muni became devoted. After Nārad came Kṛṣṇa Dvaipāyan Vedavyās. Pūrṇaprajña Madhva Āchārya declared he was Vyāsa’s servant. He was Padmanābha Tīrtha’s Guru.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nṛhari mādhava-vaṁśe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;akṣobhya paramahaṁse</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śiṣya bali’ aṅgīkāra kare</strong><br>
<strong>akṣobhyera śiṣya jaya-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tīrtha nāme parichaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā̐ra dāsye jñānasindhu tare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Nṛhari Tīrtha and Mādhava Tīrtha were also disciples in Madhva Āchārya’s line. Mādhava Tīrtha accepted the paramahaṁsa Akṣobhya Tīrtha as his disciple. Akṣobhya Tīrtha’s disciple was Jaya Tīrtha. Jaya Tīrtha’s servant was Jñānasindhu.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tā̐hā haite dayānidhi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā̐ra dāsa vidyānidhi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rājendra ha-ila tā̐hā haite</strong><br>
<strong>tā̐hāra kiṅkara jaya-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dharma nāme parichaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;paramparā jāna bhāla mate</strong></p>
<p class="translation">After Jñānasindhu came Dayānidhi. Dayānidhi’s servant was Vidyānidhi. After Vidyānidhi came Rājendra. Rājendra’s servant was Jaya Dharma. Know the paramparā properly in this way.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jayadharma-dāsye khyāti&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī puruṣottama-yati</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā̐’ha’te brahmaṇya-tīrtha sūri</strong><br>
<strong>vyāsatīrtha tā̐ra dāsa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;lakṣmīpati vyāsa-dāsa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā̐hā haite mādhavendra purī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Jaya Dharma’s renowned servant was Śrī Puruṣottam Tīrtha. After Śrī Puruṣottam Tīrtha came the learned Brahmaṇya Tīrtha. Brahmaṇya Tīrtha’s servant was Vyāsa Tīrtha. Vyās Tīrtha’s servant was Lakṣmīpati. After Lakṣmīpati came Mādhavendra Purī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mādhavendra purī-vara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śiṣya-vara śrī-īśvara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nityānanda, śrī-advaita vibhu</strong><br>
<strong>īśvara-purīke dhanya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;karilena śrī-chaitanya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jagad-guru gaura mahāprabhu</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The best disciples of the great Mādhavendra Purī were Īśvar Purī, Lord Nityānanda, and Lord Śrī Advaita. Jagad-guru Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu blessed Īśvar Purī (by accepting Īśvar Purī as His Guru.)</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mahāprabhu śrī-chaitanya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rādhā-kṛṣṇa nahe anya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rūpānuga janera jīvana</strong><br>
<strong>viśvambhara priyaṅkara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī svarūpa-dāmodara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī gosvāmī rūpa-sanātana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu is nondifferent from Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa and is the very life of Śrī Rūpa’s followers. Śrīla Svarūp Dāmodar Goswāmī, Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī Prabhu, and Śrīla Sanātan Goswāmī Prabhu are most dear to Viśvambhar.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rūpa-priya mahājana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jīva-raghunātha hana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā̐ra priya kavi kṛṣṇadāsa</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-dāsa-priya-vara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;narottama sevāpara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yā̐ra pada viśvanātha-āśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Rūpa’s dear followers are the great souls Śrī Jīva and Śrī Raghunāth. Their dear follower is Śrīla Kṛṣṇadās Kavirāj. His dearmost follower is Śrī Narottam, who is ever engaged in divine service. His feet are the aspiration of Śrī Viśvanāth Chakravartī Ṭhākur.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>viśvanātha-bhakta-sātha&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;baladeva jagannātha</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā̐ra priya śrī-bhakti-vinoda</strong><br>
<strong>mahā-bhāgavata-vara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-gaura-kiśora-vara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hari-bhajanete yā̐ra moda</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The foremost devotees of Śrīla Viśvanāth Chakravartī Ṭhākur are Śrīla Baladev Vidyābhūṣaṇ and Śrīla Jagannāth Dās Bābājī. Their dear follower is Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur. His associate is the best of the topmost devotees, Śrīla Gaura Kiśor Dās Bābājī, whose sole joy is Śrī Hari’s service.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tadanuga-mahājana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-kṛṣṇa-kīrtana-dhana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yebā dila puri jaga kāma</strong><br>
<strong>śrī vārṣabhānavī-varā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā sevya sevāparā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā̐hāra dayita-dāsa nāma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The follower of Śrīla Gaura Kiśor Dās Bābājī, the great soul Dayita Dās (Śrīla Saraswatī Ṭhākur), whose wealth is Śrī Kṛṣṇa-kīrtan, fulfilled the desires of the whole world. He is the dearmost of Śrī Vārṣabhānavī and is always engaged in Her service.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tad abhinna deha-divya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;svarūpa-rūpa-raghu-jivya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā sevya yā̐ra pādapadma</strong><br>
<strong>susiddhānta mūrti-dhara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī śrīdhara guru-vara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rupānuga-sādhu-śreya-sadma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Dayita Dās’ nondifferent divine form, the living manifestation of Śrīla Svarūp Dāmodar, Śrī Rūpa, and Śrī Raghunāth, whose lotus feet are to be served eternally, is the best of Gurus, Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj. He is the embodiment of perfect siddhānta and the ultimate refuge for the sādhus who follow Śrī Rūpa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tā̐ra priya mano ’bhīṣṭa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sthāpane sadā sacheṣṭa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakti sundara śrī govinda nāma</strong><br>
<strong>tā̐ra priya manonīta&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āchāra-prachāre rata</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī bhakti-nirmalāchārya nāma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">His dear disciple, who constantly endeavours to fulfil his desires, is Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj. Śrīla Govinda Mahārāj’s dear successor, who is devoted to practising and preaching his teachings, is Śrīla Bhakti Nirmal Āchārya Mahārāj.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ei paramparā dhana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sabe gaura-nija-jana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā̐dera uchchhiṣṭe mora kāma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All the Gurus in this succession are associates of Śrī Gaurāṅga. I pray for their remnants.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-je-anilo-prema-dhana-karuna-prachura" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Viraha-gīti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ye ānila prema-dhana karuṇā prachura</strong><br>
<strong>hena prabhu kothā gelā āchārya ṭhākura</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Where has Advaita Āchārya, the Lord who mercifully brought the wealth of divine love to this world, gone?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kā̐hā mora svarūpa rūpa kā̐hā sanātana</strong><br>
<strong>kā̐hā dāsa raghunātha patita-pāvana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Where are my Svarūp, Rūpa, and Sanātan? Where is my Raghunāth Dās? Where are the saviours of the fallen souls?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kā̐hā mora bhaṭṭa-yuga kā̐hā kavirāja</strong><br>
<strong>eka-kāle kothā gelā gorā naṭa-rāja</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Where are my Raghunāth Bhaṭṭa and Gopāl Bhaṭṭa? Where is my Kṛṣṇadās Kavirāj? Where has the great dancer Śrī Gaurāṅga suddenly gone?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pāṣāṇe kuṭiba māthā anale paśiba</strong><br>
<strong>gaurāṅga guṇera nidhi kothā gele pāba</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I will break my head on a rock, I will enter into fire—where shall I go to reach the reservoir of all qualities, Śrī Gaurāṅga?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>se saba saṅgīra saṅge ye kaila vilāsa</strong><br>
<strong>se saṅga nā pāñā kānde narottama dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Not finding the association of Śrī Gaurāṅga, who performed His Pastimes with all these associates, Narottam Dās weeps.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-pralaya-payodhi-jale-dhritavan-asi-vedam" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Daśāvatār Stotram <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Jayadev Goswāmī</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pralaya-payodhi-jale dhṛtavān asi vedaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vihita-vahitra-charitram akhedam</strong><br>
<strong>keśava dhṛta-mīna-śarīra jaya jagadīśa hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Hari! O Keśava! O Lord of the universe! You have assumed the form of a fish! All glory to You! Easily You acted like a boat and upheld the Vedas above the waters of the cosmic devastation.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṣitir iha vipulatare tiṣṭhati tava pṛṣṭhe</strong><br>
<strong>dharaṇi-dharaṇa-kina-chakra-gariṣṭhe</strong><br>
<strong>keśava dhṛta-kūrma-śarīra jaya jagadīśa hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Hari! O Keśava! O Lord of the universe! You have assumed the form of a tortoise! All glory to You! You upheld Mount Mandara upon Your giant back and thereafter became beautified by the circular scar it left.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vasati daśana-śikhare dharaṇī tava lagnā</strong><br>
<strong>śaśini kalaṅka-kaleva nimagnā</strong><br>
<strong>keśava dhṛta-śūkara-rūpa jaya jagadīśa hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Hari! O Keśava! O Lord of the universe! You have assumed the form of a boar! All glory to You! The earth sits fixed atop the tip of Your tusk like a dark spot upon the moon.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tava kara-kamala-vare nakham adbhuta-śṛṅgaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>dalita-hiraṇyakaśipu-tanu-bhṛṅgam</strong><br>
<strong>keśava dhṛta-narahari-rūpa jaya jagadīśa hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Hari! O Keśava! O Lord of the universe! You have assumed a half-man, half-lion form! All glory to You! You ripped apart the bee-like body of Hiraṇyakaśipu with the amazingly sharp nails of Your beautiful lotus hands.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chhalayasi vikramaṇe balim adbhuta-vāmana</strong><br>
<strong>pada-nakha-nīra-janita-jana-pāvana</strong><br>
<strong>keśava dhṛta-vāmana-rūpa jaya jagadīśa hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Hari! O Keśava! O Lord of the universe! You have assumed the form of a dwarf! All glory to You! In the form of an amazing dwarf You deceived Bali Mahārāj with Your giant steps and delivered all souls with the water flowing from Your toenails.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṣatriya-rudhira-maye jagad-apagata-pāpaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>snapayasi payasi śamita-bhava-tāpam</strong><br>
<strong>keśava dhṛta-bhṛgupati-rūpa jaya jagadīśa hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Hari! O Keśava! O Lord of the universe! You have assumed the form of a protector of the sages, Paraśurām! All glory to You! You bathed the world with the blood of the kṣatriyas and absolved all the world’s sins and sorrows.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vitarasi dikṣu raṇe dik-pati-kamanīyaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>daśa-mukha-mauli-baliṁ ramaṇīyam</strong><br>
<strong>keśava dhṛta-rāma-śarīra jaya jagadīśa hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Hari! O Keśava! O Lord of the universe! You have assumed the form of Rāmachandra! All glory to You! During the battle at Laṅka You distributed to all the directions the most pleasing, crowning offering of the ten heads of Rāvaṇ, which were desired by the guardians of the directions.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vahasi vapuṣi viśade vasanaṁ jaladābhaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>hala-hati-bhīti-milita jamunābham</strong><br>
<strong>keśava dhṛta-haladhara-rūpa jaya jagadīśa hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Hari! O Keśava! O Lord of the universe! You have assumed the form of Balarām! All glory to You! On Your white body You wear garments the colour of clouds and the River Yamunā when she is frightened by the striking of Your plough.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nindasi yajña-vidher ahaha śruti-jātaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>sadaya-hṛdaya darśita-paśu-ghātam</strong><br>
<strong>keśava dhṛta-buddha-śarīra jaya jagadīśa hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Hari! O Keśava! O Lord of the universe! You have assumed the form of Buddha! All glory to You! Your heart filled with compassion, You decry the scriptural injunctions for animal sacrifice.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mlechchha nivaha-nidhane kalayasi karavālaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>dhūmaketum iva kim api karālam</strong><br>
<strong>keśava dhṛta-kalki-śarīra jaya jagadīśa hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Hari! O Keśava! O Lord of the universe! You have assumed the form of Kalki! All glory to You! To destroy the wicked You brandish Your stunning, frightening, comet-like sword.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-jayadeva-kaver idam uditam udāraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>śṛṇu sukha-daṁ śubha-daṁ bhava-sāram</strong><br>
<strong>keśava dhṛta-daśa-vidha-rūpa jaya jagadīśa hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Hari! O Keśava! O Lord of the universe! You have assumed ten different forms! All glory to You! O readers, listen to this grand, pleasing, auspicious, extraordinary composition by the poet Śrī Jayadev.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vedān udharate jaganti vahate bhū-golam udbibhrate</strong><br>
<strong>daityaṁ dārayate baliṁ chhalayate kṣatra-kṣayaṁ kurvate</strong><br>
<strong>paulastyaṁ jayate halaṁ kalayate kāruṇyam ātanvate</strong><br>
<strong>mlechchhān mūrchchhayate daśākṛti-kṛte kṛṣṇāya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tubhyaṁ namaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my obeisance unto Śrī Kṛṣṇa, who assumes ten forms and rescues the Vedas, upholds Mandara Mountain, uplifts the earth, rips apart demons, deceives Bali Mahārāj, kills the kṣatriyas, conquers Rāvaṇ, carries a plough, shows compassion, and annihilates the wicked.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shri-hari-vasare-hari-kirtana-vidhana" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Harivāsar Gīti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Vṛndāvan Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī hari-vāsare hari-kīrtana-vidhāna</strong><br>
<strong>nṛtya ārambhilā prabhu jagatera prāṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">On the holy day of Śrī Hari (Ekādaśī), Śrīman Mahāprabhu, the life and soul of the universe, began dancing.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>puṇyavanta śrīvāsa-aṅgane śubhārambha</strong><br>
<strong>uṭhila kīrtana-dhvani gopāla govinda</strong></p>
<p class="translation">With the auspicious beginning of the kīrtan in the courtyard of the fortunate Śrīvās, the sound of the chanting, ‘Gopāl!’ ‘Govinda!’ arose.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mṛdaṅga mandirā bāje śaṅkha karatāla</strong><br>
<strong>saṅkīrtana saṅge saba ha-ila miśāla</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The sounds of the mṛdaṅgas, cymbals, conchshells, and clapping hands all combined together in the saṅkīrtan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>brahmāṇḍe uṭhila dhvani pūriyā ākāśa</strong><br>
<strong>chaudikera amaṅgala yāya saba nāśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The sound of the saṅkīrtan arose and filled the sky, destroying inauspiciousness in all directions.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chatur-dike śrī hari-maṅgala saṅkīrtana</strong><br>
<strong>madhye nāche jagannātha miśrera nandana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Mahāprabhu Viśvambhar, the son of Jagannāth Miśra, danced, surrounded by the auspicious Hari-nām-saṅkīrtan on all sides.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sabāra aṅgete śobhe śrī chandana-mālā</strong><br>
<strong>ānande nāchaye sabe ha-iye vibholā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Everyone was decorated with sandalwood paste and flower garlands, and danced, overwhelmed with joy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nijānande nāche mahāprabhu viśvambhara</strong><br>
<strong>charaṇera tāli śuni ati manohara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Mahāprabhu Viśvambhar danced in the ecstasy of Himself. Hearing the rhythm of His feet was heart capturing.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhāvāveśe mālā nāhi rahaye galāya</strong><br>
<strong>chhiṇḍiyā paḍaye giyā bhakatera gāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">While He was rapt in ecstasy, His flower garland could not remain around His neck. It tore and fell as the devotees sang.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yā̐ra nāmānande śiva vasana nā jāne</strong><br>
<strong>yā̐ra rase nāche śiva se nāche āpane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the ecstasy of whose Name Śiva forgets His clothing, in the ecstasy of whom Śiva always dances, that Supreme Lord now dances personally.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yā̐ra nāme vālmīki ha-ila tapodhana</strong><br>
<strong>yā̐ra nāme ajāmila pāila mochana</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yā̐ra nāme śravane saṁsāra-bandha ghuche</strong><br>
<strong>hena prabhu avatari kali-yuge nāche</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By whose Name Vālmīki became a powerful sage, by whose Name Ajāmil attained liberation, by hearing whose Name the bondage of material existence is destroyed, that very Lord descends in the Age of Kali and dances.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yā̐ra nāma la-i śuka nārada beḍāya</strong><br>
<strong>sahasra-vadana-prabhu yāra guṇa gāya</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sarva-mahā-prāyaśchita ye prabhura nāma</strong><br>
<strong>se prabhu nāchaye dekhe yata bhāgyavān</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He whose Name Śukadev and Nārad take as they wander about, whose glories the thousand-mouthed Anantadev sings, whose Name is the supreme form of atonement, that Lord now dances and glances upon the fortunate souls.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="14">
<h5>14</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī kṛṣṇa-chaitanya nityānanda-chā̐da</strong><br>
<strong>jāna vṛndāvana dāsa tachhu pada-yuge gāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Embracing Śrī Kṛṣṇa Chaitanya and Nityānandachā̐d as his life and soul, Vṛndāvan Dās sings the glories of Their lotus feet.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shiuddha-bhakata-charana-renu" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śuddha-bhakata <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śuddha-bhakata-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;charaṇa-reṇu</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhajana-anukūla</strong><br>
<strong>bhakata-sevā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;parama-siddhi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prema-latikāra mūla</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The dust of pure devotees’ feet is favourable to devotion. Serving the devotees is the supreme fulfilment and the root of the creeper of divine love.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mādhava-tithi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakti-jananī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yatane pālana kari</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-vasati&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vasati bali’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;parama ādare vari</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I carefully observe the holy days related to Mādhava. They are the mother of devotion. With great adoration, I embrace the abode of Kṛṣṇa as my own.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaura āmāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ye-saba sthāne</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;karala bhramaṇa raṅge</strong><br>
<strong>se-saba sthāna&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;heriba āmi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;praṇayi-bhakata-saṅge</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the company of loving devotees, I behold all the places my Gaurasundar visited during His Pastimes.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mṛdaṅga-vādya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śunite mana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;avasara sadā yāche</strong><br>
<strong>gaura-vihita&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kīrtana śuni’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ānande hṛdaya nāche</strong></p>
<p class="translation">My heart always begs for the opportunity to hear the sound of the mṛdaṅga. Hearing the kīrtan prescribed by Śrī Gaurasundar, my heart dances with joy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yugala-mūrti&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dekhiyā mora</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;parama-ānanda haya</strong><br>
<strong>prasāda-sevā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;karite haya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sakala prapañcha jaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Beholding the Deity forms of the Divine Couple, I feel the greatest joy. By honouring prasād, I overcome the entire material illusion.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ye-dina gṛhe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhajana dekhi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gṛhete goloka bhāya</strong><br>
<strong>charaṇa-sīdhu&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dekhiyā gaṅgā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sukha nā sīmā pāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">On days when I see devotional service taking place in my home, I feel that Goloka has manifested there. Seeing the Ganges, the nectar from the Lord’s feet, my happiness has no limit.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tulasī dekhi’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;juḍāya prāṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mādhava-toṣaṇī jāni’</strong><br>
<strong>gaura-priya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śāka-sevane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jīvana sārthaka māni</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Seeing Tulasī Devī soothes my soul, for I know she pleases Mādhava. Honouring śāk, Śrī Gaurasundar’s favourite, I consider my life successful.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakativinoda&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa-bhajane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;anukūla pāya yāhā</strong><br>
<strong>prati-divase&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;parama-sukhe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;svīkāra karaye tāhā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Whatever Bhakti Vinod obtains that is favourable to Kṛṣṇa’s service, he accepts every day with the greatest joy.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-radhe-jaya-jaya-madhava-dayite" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>After parikramā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī Prabhu</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>nagara bhramiyā āmāra gaura ela ghare</strong><br>
<strong>gaura ela ghare āmāra nitāi ela ghare</strong><br>
<strong>dhūlā jhāri’ śachīmātā gaura kole kare</strong><br>
<strong>ānandete bhakta-gaṇe hari hari bale</strong></p>
<p class="translation">After touring the town, my Gaurāṅga came home! My Gaurāṅga and my Nitāi came home! After dusting Him off, Śachī Mātā embraces Gaurāṅga! The devotees joyfully sing “Hari! Hari!”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhe jaya jaya mādhava-dayite</strong><br>
<strong>gokula-taruṇī-maṇḍala-mahite</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Rādhā! O Beloved of Mādhava! O heroine of the girls of Gokula! All glory to You! All glory to You!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dāmodara-rati-vardhana-veśe</strong><br>
<strong>hari-niṣkuṭa-vṛndā-vipineśe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O She who dresses to increase Dāmodar’s attachment! O Queen of Hari’s pleasure groves in Vṛndāvan!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vṛṣabhānūdadhi-nava-śaśi-lekhe</strong><br>
<strong>lalitā-sakhi guṇa-ramita-viśākhe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O new moon who has arisen from the ocean of King Vṛṣabhānu! O friend of Lalitā! O charmer of Viśākhā!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>karuṇāṁ kuru mayi karuṇā-bharite</strong><br>
<strong>sanaka-sanātana-varṇita-charite</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Be merciful to me, O You who are filled with mercy! O She whose glory is sung by Sanaka and Sanātan!</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-hari-haraye-namah-krishna-yadavaya-namah" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Nām-saṅkīrtan <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari haraye namaḥ kṛṣṇa yādavāya namaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>yādavāya mādhavāya keśavāya namaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord, Kṛṣṇa, Yādava, Hari, Mādhava, Keśava, again and again I offer my obeisance to You.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gopāla govinda rāma śrī madhusūdana</strong><br>
<strong>giridhārī gopīnātha madana-mohana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to Gopāl, Govinda, Rām, Śrī Madhusūdan, Giridhārī, Gopīnāth, Madan Mohan!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-chaitanya nityānanda śrī advaitachandra</strong><br>
<strong>gadādhara śrīvāsādi gaura-bhakta-vṛnda</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to Śrī Chaitanya, Nityānanda, Advaitachandra, Gadādhar, Śrivās, and all the Lord’s devotees!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya rūpa sanātana bhaṭṭa raghunātha</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-jīva gopāla-bhaṭṭa dāsa raghunātha</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to Śrī Rūpa, Sanātan, Raghunāth Bhaṭṭa, Śrī Jīva, Gopāl Bhaṭṭa, and Raghunāth Dās!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ei chhaya gosāñi kari charaṇa vandana</strong><br>
<strong>yāhā haite vighna-nāśa abhīṣṭa-pūraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my obeisance to these six goswāmīs. By doing so, all obstacles are destroyed and all desires are fulfilled.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ei chhaya gosāñi yā̐ra mu̐i tā̐ra dāsa</strong><br>
<strong>tā̐-sabāra pada-reṇu mora pañcha-grāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I am a servant of these six goswāmīs. Their foot dust is my<br>
five forms of sustenance.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tā̐dera charaṇa sevi bhakta sane vāsa</strong><br>
<strong>janame janame mora ei abhilāṣa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">To live with the devotees who serve their feet is my desire, birth after birth.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ei chhaya gosāñi yabe vraje kailā vāsa</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-kṛṣṇa-nitya-līlā karilā prakāśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When these six goswāmīs lived in Vraja, they revealed Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa’s eternal Pastimes.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ānande bala hari bhaja vṛndāvana</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-guru-vaiṣṇava-pade majāiyā mana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Joyfully chant, ‘Hari!’, and worship Vṛndāvan, meditating on the feet of Śrī Guru and the Vaiṣṇavas.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-guru-vaiṣṇava-pāda-padma kari āśa</strong><br>
<strong>(hari) nāma-saṅkīrtana kahe narottama dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Aspiring for the lotus feet of Śrī Guru and the Vaiṣṇavas, Narottam Dās chants the Holy Name.</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
